## IAH-ʽEN DJÜN FOH-ING SHÜ. <u>約翰</u>傳福音書

### Iʽe. I.

<ruby><rb>道</rb><rt>DAO</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>起初</rb><rt>kyʽi-tsʽu</rt><rb>有個</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>道</rb><rt>Dao</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>Dao</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>起初</rb><rt>kyʽi-tsʽu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>萬物</rb><rt>Væn-veh</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>託</rb><rt>tʽôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>造個</rb><rt>zao-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>造出來個</rb><rt>zao-cʽih-læ-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>造個</rb><rt>zao-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>活命</rb><rt>Weh-ming</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>在於</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>亮光</rb><rt>liang-kwông</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>亮光</rb><rt>liang-kwông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>暗個</rb><rt>en-go</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>照東</rb><rt>tsiao-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>暗個</rb><rt>en-go</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>差來個</rb><rt>tsʽa-læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>亮光個</rb><rt>Liang-kwông-go</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>*te-tsing*</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>亮光</rb><rt>Liang-kwông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>亮光個</rb><rt>Liang-kwông-go</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>*læ-go*</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>真個</rb><rt>Tsing-go</rt><rb>亮光</rb><rt>Liang-kwông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>亮光</rb><rt>liang-kwông</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>託</rb><rt>tʽôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>造個</rb><rt>zao-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>認得</rb><rt>nying-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>Zi-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>*di-fông*</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其自個</rb><rt>Gyi-zi-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>能幹</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>兒囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>生出來</rb><rt>*sang-cʽih-læ*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>血脈</rb><rt>hyüih-mah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>肉身個</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing-go</rt><rb>主意</rb><rt>cü-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>主意</rb><rt>cü-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>生出來</rb><rt>sang-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Y. ʽE. 26. 耶（穌）後（頭）26（年）。（原註）</sup>

<sup>\[2\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, gyün-ping. 或話，權柄。（原註）</sup>

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>Dao</rt><rb>成</rb><rt>dzing</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，（<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>親眼</rb><rt>*tsʽing-ngæn*</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>天父</rb><rt>*Tʽin-Vu*</rt><rb>差來</rb><rt>tsʽa-læ</rt><rb>獨養</rb><rt>doh-yiang</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>*Ng-ts*</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>；）<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>滿足</rb><rt>mun-tsoh</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>響響</rb><rt>hyiang-hyiang</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>後如</rb><rt>ʽEo-jü</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>尊重了</rb><rt>tseng-djong-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>先如</rb><rt>sin-jü</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>來東個</rb><rt>læ-tong-go</rt></ruby>。』」<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>Dzong</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>滿足</rb><rt>mun-tsoh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>*eng-we*</rt><rb>得着了</rb><rt>teh-djôh-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>*teh-djôh*</rt><rb>恩上</rb><rt>eng-zông</rt><rb>加</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>恩</rb><rt>eng</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>託</rb><rt>tʽôh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>傳個</rb><rt>djün-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>託</rb><rt>tʽôh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>Dzong</rt><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>過</rb><rt>ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>胸脘頭</rb><rt>hyüong-kwun-deo</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>獨養</rb><rt>doh-yiang</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>顯出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt><rb>來了</rb><rt>læ-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>19</sup> <u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>YIU-TʽA</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>，「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>約翰</u>個</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen-go</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>介個</rb><rt>ka-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>招認</rb><rt>tsiao-jing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>賴</rb><rt>la</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>招認</rb><rt>tsiao-jing</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>*z-ka*</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>。」<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亞</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt></ruby>。」「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>Feh-z</rt></ruby>。」<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>到底</rb><rt>tao-ti</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>回報</rb><rt>we-pao</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？」<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>Ziang</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>sin-cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以賽亞</rb><rt>Yi-sæ-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>曠野</rb><rt>Kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>聲響</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>*z-ka*</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>鋪直</rb><rt>pʽu-dzih</rt><rb>主個</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt></ruby>。』 <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>*keh-go*</rt><rb>聲響</rb><rt>*sing-hyiang*</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] Y. ʽE. 30. 耶（穌）後（頭）30（年）。（原註）</sup>

<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>差來個</rb><rt>tsʽa-læ-go</rt><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利賽</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亞</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>？」<sup>26</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>認得個</rb><rt>nying-teh-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>後如</rb><rt>ʽeo-jü</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>尊重了</rb><rt>tseng-djong-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>解</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>鞋帶</rb><rt>ʽa-ta</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>欠</rb><rt>kyʽin</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河外</rb><rt>ʽo-nga</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯大巴喇</rb><rt>Pah-da-pô-lah</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>DI-NYI</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>且看</rb><rt>Tsʽia-kʽen</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>Siao-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>世間上個</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>頂勒去個</rb><rt>ting-leh-kyʽi-go</rt></ruby>！<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>後如</rb><rt>ʽeo-jü</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>尊重了</rb><rt>tseng-djong-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>先如</rb><rt>sin-jü</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>來東個</rb><rt>læ-tong-go</rt></ruby>。』<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>*zin-deo*</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>特爲</rb><rt>deh-we</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>顯</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>出來</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>32</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>鵓鴿</rb><rt>beh-keh</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天裏</rb><rt>tʽin-li</rt><rb>降落來</rb><rt>kông-lôh-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>上頭</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling</rt><rb>降落</rb><rt>kông-lôh</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>上頭</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。』<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>看見過了</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-ko-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>Di-nyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>立間</rb><rt>lih-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>*dô-kô*</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>*læ-kæn*</rt></ruby>。<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>且看</rb><rt>Tsʽia-kʽen</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>Siao-yiang</rt></ruby>！」<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>keng-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>38</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>扭轉</rb><rt>nyin-cün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>跟的</rb><rt>keng-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>啥西</rb><rt>soh-si</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>拉比</rb><rt>Lah-pi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>庉間</rb><rt>deng-kæn</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>？」（<ruby><rb>拉比個</rb><rt>Lah-pi-go</rt><rb>解說</rb><rt>ka-shih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt></ruby>。）<sup>39</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>庉個</rb><rt>deng-go</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>庉過</rb><rt>deng-ko</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>下半日</rb><rt>ʽô-pun-nyih</rt><rb>毛</rb><rt>mao</rt><rb>四</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>點鐘</rb><rt>tin-cong</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, tæn-kôh-kæn. 或話，耽擱間。（原註）</sup>

<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>兩</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>約翰</u>個</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>跟</rb><rt>keng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西門</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u><u>·</u><ruby><rb><u>彼得</u>個</rb><rt>Pe-teh-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>安得烈</rb><rt>En-teh-lih</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>41</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>尋着</rb><rt>zing-djôh</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西門</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>彌賽亞</rb><rt>Mi-sæ-üô</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>尋着了</rb><rt>zing-djôh-de</rt></ruby>。」（<ruby><rb>彌賽亞</rb><rt>Mi-sæ-üô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>翻出來</rb><rt>fæn-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>。）<sup>42</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>見</rb><rt>kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>。<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>約拿</u>個</rb><rt>Iah-nô-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西門</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>磯法</rb><rt>Kyi-fah</rt></ruby></u>。」（<u><ruby><rb>磯法</rb><rt>Kyi-fah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>翻出來</rb><rt>fæn-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>。）

<sup>\[1\] Kyi-toh *go* *ka-shih*, *ziu-z* fu-yiu Cü-kwu. <u>基督</u>個解說，就是傅油主顧。（原註）</sup>

<sup>\[2\] Pe-teh *go* *ka-shih*, *ziu-z* zah-deo. <u>彼得</u>個解說，就是石頭。（原註）</sup>

<sup>43</sup> <ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>DI-NYI</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>打賬</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尋着</rb><rt>zing-djôh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>腓力</rb><rt>Fi-lih</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>跟</rb><rt>Keng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。」<sup>44</sup> <u><ruby><rb>腓力</rb><rt>Fi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯賽大</rb><rt>Pah-sæ-da</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>安得烈</rb><rt>En-teh-lih</rt></ruby></u>、<ruby><rb><u>彼得</u>個</rb><rt>Pe-teh-go</rt><rb>本城</rb><rt>peng-dzing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>45</sup> <u><ruby><rb>腓力</rb><rt>Fi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>尋着</rb><rt>zing-djôh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拿但業</rb><rt>Nô-dæn-nyih</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>話個</rb><rt>wô-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>尋着了</rb><rt>zing-djôh-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拿撒勒</rb><rt>Nô-sah-leh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>約瑟</u>個</rb><rt>Iah-seh-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>。」<sup>46</sup> <u><ruby><rb>拿但業</rb><rt>Nô-dæn-nyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<u><ruby><rb>拿撒勒</rb><rt>Nô-sah-leh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>好個</rb><rt>hao-go</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<u><ruby><rb>腓力</rb><rt>Fi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>47</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拿但業</rb><rt>Nô-dæn-nyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>點着</rb><rt>tin-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>且看</rb><rt>Tsʽia-kʽen</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>實惠</rb><rt>jih-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>奸詐個</rb><rt>kæn-tsô-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>48</sup> <u><ruby><rb>拿但業</rb><rt>Nô-dæn-nyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>認得</rb><rt>nying-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>？」<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<u><ruby><rb>腓力</rb><rt>Fi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>無花果</rb><rt>vu-hwô-ko</rt><rb>樹下</rb><rt>jü-ʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>過了</rb><rt>ko-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>49</sup> <u><ruby><rb>拿但業</rb><rt>Nô-dæn-nyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>夫子</rb><rt>Fu-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以色列</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>Wông-ti</rt></ruby>。」<sup>50</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>『<ruby><rb>無花果</rb><rt>Vu-hwô-ko</rt><rb>樹下</rb><rt>jü-ʽô</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>過了</rb><rt>ko-de</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>相信個</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>大如</rb><rt>do-jü</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看見其</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-gyi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>51</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真真</rb><rt>tsing-tsing</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從今以後</rb><rt>Dzong-kying-yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>開開</rb><rt>kʽæ-kʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>人個兒子</rb><rt>Nying-go-Ng-ts</rt><rb>上頭</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt><rb>走落</rb><rt>tseo-lôh</rt></ruby>。」

---

### Iʽe. II.

<ruby><rb>第三</rb><rt>DI-SÆN</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>加利利</u>個</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦拿</rb><rt>Kyüô-nô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一份</rb><rt>ih-veng</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>好日</rb><rt>hao-nyih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb><u>耶穌</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>請去間</rb><rt>tsʽing-kyʽi-kæn</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>好日酒</rb><rt>hao-nyih-tsiu</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>酒</rb><rt>Tsiu</rt><rb>欠了</rb><rt>kyʽin-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>耶穌</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt><rb>嘸得了</rb><rt>m-neh-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>4</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>Nyü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>相干</rb><rt>siang-ken</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>傭人</rb><rt>yüong-nying</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>話個</rb><rt>wô-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>Tsiao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>弄</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>潔淨</rb><rt>kyih-zing</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>規矩</rb><rt>kwe-kyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>六口</rb><rt>loh-kʽeo</rt><rb>石水缸</rb><rt>zah-shü-kông</rt><rb>安間</rb><rt>en-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>每口</rb><rt>me-kʽeo</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>𣥼</rb><rt>tsi</rt><rb>兩三擔</rb><rt>liang-sæn-tæn</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>*shü*</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>傭人</rb><rt>yüong-nying</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>𣥼滿</rb><rt>tsi-mun</rt><rb>水缸</rb><rt>shü-kông</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>候沿</rb><rt>ʽeo-yin</rt><rb>𣥼滿了</rb><rt>tsi-mun-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>舀出來</rb><rt>iao-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>馱去</rb><rt>do-kyʽi</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>總管</rb><rt>tsong-kwun</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>馱去了</rb><rt>do-kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>變</rb><rt>pin</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>總管</rb><rt>tsong-kwun</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>嚐着</rb><rt>zông-djôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt></ruby>，（<ruby><rb>舀</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>傭人</rb><rt>yüong-nying</rt><rb>曉得個</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt></ruby>，）<ruby><rb>總管</rb><rt>tsong-kwun</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>新郎</rb><rt>sing-lông</rt></ruby>，<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>人家</rb><rt>Nying-kô</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>擺</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>喫足快</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-tsoh-kwʽa</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>推扳點</rb><rt>tʽe-pæn-tin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>反轉</rb><rt>*fæn-cün*</rt><rb>留</rb><rt>liu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>頭一起</rb><rt>deo-ih-kyʽi</rt><rb>神蹟</rb><rt>jing-tsih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>加利利</u>個</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦拿</rb><rt>Kyüô-nô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>*z-ka*</rt><rb>顯出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>走落</rb><rt>tseo-lôh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦百農</rb><rt>Kyüô-pah-nong</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>庉落</rb><rt>deng-lôh</rt><rb>嘸幾</rb><rt>m-kyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>YIU-TʽA</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>逾越節</rb><rt>Yü-yüih-tsih</rt><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-de</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>聖殿裏</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>賣</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>鵓鴿</rb><rt>beh-keh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>兌</rb><rt>de</rt><rb>銅鈿</rb><rt>dong-din</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>坐間</rb><rt>zo-kæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>繩</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>鞭子</rb><rt>pin-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>趕出</rb><rt>ken-cʽih</rt><rb>聖殿</rb><rt>sing-din</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>倒掉</rb><rt>tao-diao</rt><rb>兌</rb><rt>de</rt><rb>銅鈿</rb><rt>dong-din</rt><rb>主顧個</rb><rt>cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>翻倒</rb><rt>fæn-tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>櫃頭</rb><rt>gyü-deo</rt></ruby>，<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>賣</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>鵓鴿</rb><rt>beh-keh</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>馱勒去</rb><rt>do-leh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>阿爹個</rb><rt>Ah-tia-go</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>買賣個</rb><rt>ma-ma-go</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>記得</rb><rt>kyi-teh</rt><rb>書裏</rb><rt>*Shü-li*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>寫的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>着急煞</rb><rt>dziah-kyih-sah</rt></ruby>。」 

<sup>18</sup> <u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>神蹟</rb><rt>jing-tsih</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>？」<sup>19</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>拆掉</rb><rt>tsʽah-diao</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>聖殿</rb><rt>*sing*-din</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>起</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>。」<sup>20</sup> <u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>造</rb><rt>Zao</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>殿</rb><rt>din</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>管</rb><rt>kwun</rt><rb>四十</rb><rt>s-jih</rt><rb>六</rb><rt>loh</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>難道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>起</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇句</rb><rt>*keh-kyü*</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>*shih-wô*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>指點</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>肌身個</rb><rt>kyi-sing-go</rt><rb>殿</rb><rt>din</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>活轉來</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt><rb>之後</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>纔方</rb><rt>*dzæ-fông*</rt><rb>記得到</rb><rt>kyi-teh-tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>箇句</rb><rt>keh-kyü</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>講過</rb><rt>kông-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>聖書</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。

<sup>23</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>逾越</rb><rt>Yü-yüih</rt><rb>節肯</rb><rt>tsih-kʽeng</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>做出來個</rb><rt>tso-cʽih-læ-go</rt><rb>神蹟</rb><rt>jing-tsih</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>靠託</rb><rt>kʽao-tʽôh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>*i-s*</rt></ruby>，<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>心意</rb><rt>sing-i</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>Gyi-zi</rt><rb>曉得個</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt></ruby>。

### Iʽe. III.

<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利賽</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>尼哥底母</rb><rt>Nyi-ko-ti-meo</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>官</rb><rt>kwun</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>夜到</rb><rt>yia-tao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>見</rb><rt>kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>夫子</rb><rt>Fu-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>神蹟</rb><rt>jing-tsih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真真</rb><rt>tsing-tsing</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一個</rb><rt>Ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>重生出來</rb><rt>djong-sang-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>國</rb><rt>koh</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] Yiu-teh *fæn*, dzong zông-deo ka sang-go. 有得翻，從上頭介生個。（原註）</sup>

<sup>4</sup> <u><ruby><rb>尼哥底母</rb><rt>Nyi-ko-ti-meo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>老了</rb><rt>lao-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>生出來</rb><rt>sang-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>難道</rb><rt>Næn-dao</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>重入</rb><rt>djong-jih</rt><rb>孃胎</rb><rt>nyiang-tʽæ</rt><rb>生出來</rb><rt>sang-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>5</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>應起</rb><rt>ing-kyʽi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真真</rb><rt>tsing-tsing</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一個</rb><rt>Ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>生出來</rb><rt>sang-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>國</rb><rt>koh</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>Dzong</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt><rb>生個</rb><rt>sang-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling</rt><rb>生個</rb><rt>sang-go</rt><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>靈</rb><rt>ling</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>風</rb><rt>Fong</rt><rb>隨</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>吹</rb><rt>cʽü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>聲響</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>生個</rb><rt>sang-go</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] Yiu-teh *fæn*, dzong zông-deo ka sang-go. 有得翻，從上頭介生個。（原註）</sup>

<sup>9</sup> <u><ruby><rb>尼哥底母</rb><rt>Nyi-ko-ti-meo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>做勒來</rb><rt>tso-leh-læ</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>！<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真真</rb><rt>tsing-tsing</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>曉得個</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>看見過</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-ko</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>阿拉個</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>除了</rb><rt>Djü-leh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>降落來個</rb><rt>kông-lôh-læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt><rb>來東個</rb><rt>læ-tong-go</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>之外</rb><rt>ts-nga</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>升上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>過</rb><rt>ko</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>Tsing-ziang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇梗</rb><rt>keh-kwang</rt><rb>蛇</rb><rt>dzô</rt><rb>提上去</rb><rt>di-zông-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>提上去</rb><rt>di-zông-kyʽi</rt></ruby>；<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>滅掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>永遠</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt></ruby>。

<sup>16</sup> 「<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>愛惜</rb><rt>æ-sih</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>甚至於</rb><rt>zing-ts-ü</rt><rb>賜落</rb><rt>s-lôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>獨養</rb><rt>doh-yiang</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>滅掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>永遠</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>定</rb><rt>ding</rt><rb>世間上個</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>相信</rb><rt>Siang-sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>定罪</rb><rt>ding-ze</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信個</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>定罪</rb><rt>ding-ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>獨養</rb><rt>doh-yiang</rt><rb>兒子個</rb><rt>Ng-ts-go</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>*Gyi-lah*</rt><rb>定罪個</rb><rt>ding-ze-go</rt><rb>緣故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>：<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>亮光</rb><rt>liang-kwông</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>暗</rb><rt>en</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>過如</rb><rt>ko-jü</rt><rb>亮光</rb><rt>liang-kwông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>行爲</rb><rt>ʽang-we</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>惡個</rb><rt>ôh-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>非禮</rb><rt>fi-li</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>惡愫</rb><rt>u-su</rt><rb>亮光</rb><rt>liang-kwông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>亮光</rb><rt>liang-kwông</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>責罰</rb><rt>tsah-vah</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>行爲</rb><rt>ʽang-we</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>亮光</rb><rt>liang-kwông</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>顯出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>行爲</rb><rt>ʽang-we</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>在於</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>KEH-SING</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>耽擱</rb><rt>tæn-kôh</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒冷</rb><rt>Sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>相近個</rb><rt>siang-gying-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哀嫩</rb><rt>Æ-neng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>*Keh-go*</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>*z-ʽeo*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>挈落</rb><rt>kyʽih-lôh</rt><rb>牢監</rb><rt>lao-kæn</rt></ruby>。

<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb><u>約翰</u>個</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>扳駁</rb><rt>pæn-poh</rt><rb>弄</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>潔淨</rb><rt>kyih-zing</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>規矩</rb><rt>kwe-kyü</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>夫子</rb><rt>Fu-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河外</rb><rt>ʽo-nga</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>27</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>話過了</rb><rt>wô-ko-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>不過</rb><rt>peh-ko</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>差來個</rb><rt>tsʽa-læ-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>新來新婦</rb><rt>sing-læ-sing-vu</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>新郎</rb><rt>sing-lông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>新郎個</rb><rt>sing-lông-go</rt><rb>朋友</rb><rt>beng-yiu</rt><rb>立勒</rb><rt>lih-leh</rt><rb>聽東</rb><rt>tʽing-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>新郎個</rb><rt>sing-lông-go</rt><rb>聲音</rb><rt>sing-ing</rt><rb>大樣</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>難間</rb><rt>*næn-kæn*</rt><rb>滿足了</rb><rt>mun-tsoh-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>大起來</rb><rt>do-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>小攏去</rb><rt>siao-long-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>Dzong</rt><rb>上頭</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>凡百個</rb><rt>væn-pah-go</rt><rb>上頭</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>出於</rb><rt>cʽih-ü</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>屬於</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>屬於</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>凡百個</rb><rt>væn-pah-go</rt><rb>上頭</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>聽聞個</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>嘸人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>受</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>一顆</rb><rt>ih-kʽo</rt><rb>印</rb><rt>ing</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>表明</rb><rt>piao-ming</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>真個</rb><rt>tsing-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>差來個</rb><rt>tsʽa-læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*Gyi*</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>嘸窮盡個</rb><rt>m-gyüong-dzing-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>天父</rb><rt>*Tʽin-Vu*</rt><rb>愛惜</rb><rt>æ-sih</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>萬物</rb><rt>væn-veh</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>相信</rb><rt>Siang-sing</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>永遠</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>𢛟惶</rb><rt>ô-wông</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>上頭</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt></ruby>。」
