### Iʽe. IV.

<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利賽</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>收</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>多如</rb><rt>to-jü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u>，<sup>2</sup> （<ruby><rb>實爲</rb><rt>jih-we</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>。）<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>歸</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>路</rb><rt>Lu</rt><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>過</rb><rt>ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒瑪利亞</rb><rt>Sah-mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>到了</rb><rt>Tao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒瑪利亞</rb><rt>Sah-mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>城裏</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>敘加</rb><rt>Jü-kyüô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇塊</rb><rt>keh-kwʽe</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>相近個</rb><rt>siang-gying-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>Keh-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一口</rb><rt>ih-kʽeo</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>雅各</u>個</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh-go</rt><rb>井</rb><rt>tsing</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt><rb>走勒</rb><rt>tseo-leh</rt><rb>喫力了</rb><rt>kyʽih-lih-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是介</rb><rt>ziu-z-ka</rt><rb>坐落</rb><rt>zo-lôh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇口</rb><rt>keh-kʽeo</rt><rb>井</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>邊沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>約來</rb><rt>iah-læ</rt><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>點鐘</rb><rt>tin-cong</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒瑪利亞</rb><rt>Sah-mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>水</rb><rt>Shü</rt><rb>依點</rb><rt>i-tin</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>喫喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。」（<ruby><rb>門徒</rb><rt>Meng-du</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>城裏</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>買</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>喫食</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-zih</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。）<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒瑪利亞</rb><rt>Sah-mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒瑪利亞</rb><rt>Sah-mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>討</rb><rt>tʽao</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」（<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒瑪利亞</rb><rt>Sah-mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>來去個</rb><rt>læ-kyʽi-go</rt></ruby>。）

<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>恩賜</rb><rt>eng-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>『<ruby><rb>依點</rb><rt>I-tin</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>喫喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>』<ruby><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>Jü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>弗然</rb><rt>feh-jün</rt><rb>討</rb><rt>tʽao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>活</rb><rt>weh</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>Nyü-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>先生</rb><rt>Sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>吊樑桶</rb><rt>tiao-liang-dong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>井</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>*ka*</rt><rb>深</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>活</rb><rt>weh</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>箇口</rb><rt>Keh-kʽeo</rt><rb>井</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>難道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>大如</rb><rt>do-jü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>口渴</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeh</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>永遠</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>口渴</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>水源</rb><rt>shü-nyün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>滮出</rb><rt>piao-cʽih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>永遠</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt></ruby>。」<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>Nyü-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>先生</rb><rt>Sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>𣍐</rb><rt>ve</rt><rb>口渴</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>省勒</rb><rt>sæn-leh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>*shü*</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>16</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>Kyʽi</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>男𡢿</rb><rt>nen-nyüing</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>Nyü-nying</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>男𡢿</rb><rt>nen-nyüing</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>男𡢿</rb><rt>nen-nyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>男𡢿</rb><rt>nen-nyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有個</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>爾自個</rb><rt>ng-zi-go</rt><rb>男𡢿</rb><rt>nen-nyüing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇句</rb><rt>keh-kyü</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>講勒</rb><rt>kông-leh</rt><rb>實惠個</rb><rt>jih-we-go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>Nyü-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>先生</rb><rt>Sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇座</rb><rt>keh-zo</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>禮拜</rb><rt>li-pa</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>當該</rb><rt>tông-kæ</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>禮拜</rb><rt>li-pa</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>21</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>Nyü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>來其</rb><rt>læ-gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇座</rb><rt>keh-zo</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>天父</rb><rt>*Tʽin*-Vu</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>拜個</rb><rt>pa-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>曉得個</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>救星</rb><rt>kyiu-sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>出於</rb><rt>cʽih-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>時候</rb><rt>Z-ʽeo</rt><rb>來其</rb><rt>læ-gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>現在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>就是了</rb><rt>ziu-z-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>真真</rb><rt>tsing-tsing</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>禮拜</rb><rt>li-pa</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>靈性</rb><rt>ling-sing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>誠實</rb><rt>dzing-jih</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>天父</rb><rt>*Tʽin*-Vu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>天父</rb><rt>*Tʽin*-Vu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>箇株介</rb><rt>keh-cü-ka</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>靈</rb><rt>ling</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>當該</rb><rt>tông-kæ</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>靈性</rb><rt>ling-sing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>誠實</rb><rt>dzing-jih</rt><rb>拜個</rb><rt>pa-go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>Nyü-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>彌賽亞</rb><rt>Mi-sæ-üô</rt><rb>來其</rb><rt>læ-gyi</rt></ruby>。（<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>。）<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>樣樣</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。」<sup>26</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>扣扣</rb><rt>Kʽeo-kʽeo</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>來了</rb><rt>læ-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>奇怪</rb><rt>gyi-kwa</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>嘸人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>啥西</rb><rt>soh-si</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>啥個</rb><rt>Soh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>？」<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>Nyü-nying</rt><rb>放勒</rb><rt>fông-leh</rt><rb>水桶</rb><rt>shü-dong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>城裏</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<sup>29</sup> 「<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>道了</rb><rt>dao-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>豈</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>？」<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>*Keh-sing*</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>*nying*</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>城裏</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>Nyü-nying</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>之後</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>Wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>夫子</rb><rt>Fu-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。」<sup>32</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>口糧</rb><rt>kʽeo-liang</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得個</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>門徒</rb><rt>Meng-du</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>難道</rb><rt>Næn-dao</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>馱來</rb><rt>do-læ</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<sup>34</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>依順</rb><rt>I-jing</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>主顧個</rb><rt>Cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>旨意</rb><rt>ts-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>完全</rb><rt>wun-djün</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>口糧</rb><rt>kʽeo-liang</rt></ruby>。<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>Tsæ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>四個</rb><rt>s-ko</rt><rb>月</rb><rt>yüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>收割個</rb><rt>siu-keh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-de</rt></ruby>？』<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>抬帶起</rb><rt>dæ-tæn-kyʽi</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>田頭</rb><rt>din-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>白了</rb><rt>bah-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>收割了</rb><rt>siu-keh-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>收割個</rb><rt>Siu-keh-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>工鈿</rb><rt>kong-din</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>收拾</rb><rt>siu-jih</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>永遠</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>下秧個</rb><rt>ʽô-iang-go</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>收割個</rb><rt>siu-keh-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>快落</rb><rt>kwʽa-lôh</rt></ruby>。<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>Z-ka</rt><rb>箇句</rb><rt>keh-kyü</rt><rb>實惠個</rb><rt>jih-we-go</rt><rb>俗話</rb><rt>dzoh-wô</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>一個</rb><rt>Ih-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>下秧個</rb><rt>ʽô-iang-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>收割個</rb><rt>siu-keh-go</rt></ruby>。』<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>勞碌</rb><rt>lao-loh</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>烏蕩</rb><rt>*u-dông*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>收割</rb><rt>siu-keh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>別人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt><rb>勞碌過了</rb><rt>lao-loh-ko-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>收</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>勞碌個</rb><rt>lao-loh-go</rt><rb>陶成</rb><rt>*dao-dzing*</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>39</sup> <ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>道了</rb><rt>dao-de</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>城裏</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒瑪利亞</rb><rt>Sah-mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>40</sup> <u><ruby><rb>撒瑪利亞</rb><rt>Sah-mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>庉東</rb><rt>deng-tong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>庉上</rb><rt>deng-zông</rt><rb>兩</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>。<sup>41</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其自個</rb><rt>Gyi-zi-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>多了</rb><rt>to-de</rt></ruby>，<sup>42</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>聽聞了</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>實在</rb><rt>jih-dzæ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>世間上個</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông-go</rt><rb>救主</rb><rt>Kyiu-cü</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>43</sup> <ruby><rb>過了</rb><rt>KO-LEH</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>兩</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>歸</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>44</sup> <ruby><rb>據</rb><rt>Kyü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>先知人</rb><rt>Sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其自個</rb><rt>gyi-zi-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>嘸告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>體面</rb><rt>tʽi-min</rt></ruby>。」<sup>45</sup> <ruby><rb>走</rb><rt>Tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>節肯</rb><rt>tsih-kʽeng</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>迎接</rb><rt>nying-tsih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>守</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>節肯</rb><rt>tsih-kʽeng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>46</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>加利利</u>個</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦拿</rb><rt>Kyüô-nô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>變</rb><rt>pin</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>國王</rb><rt>koh-wông</rt><rb>手下</rb><rt>siu-ʽô</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>官</rb><rt>kwun</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦百農</rb><rt>Kyüô-pah-nong</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>生病間</rb><rt>sang-bing-kæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>47</sup> <ruby><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來東了</rb><rt>læ-tong-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>見</rb><rt>kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>落來</rb><rt>lôh-læ</rt><rb>醫</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>兒子個</rb><rt>ng-ts-go</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>*bing*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>48</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>神蹟</rb><rt>jing-tsih</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>奇事</rb><rt>gyi-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>總</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>。」<sup>49</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>官</rb><rt>kwun</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>落去</rb><rt>lôh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>50</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>活間了</rb><rt>weh-kæn-de</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] ʽÔh-wô, ih-go do kwun-fu. 或話，一個官府。（原註）</sup>

<sup>51</sup> <ruby><rb>落去</rb><rt>Lôh-kyʽi</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>鬥上來</rb><rt>teo-zông-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>活了</rb><rt>weh-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>52</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>瘥個</rb><rt>tsʽô-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>昨日</rb><rt>Zô-nyih</rt><rb>下半日</rb><rt>ʽô-pun-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>點鐘</rb><rt>tin-cong</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>熱</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>退了</rb><rt>tʽe-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>53</sup> <ruby><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>扣扣</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>「<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>活間了</rb><rt>weh-kæn-de</rt></ruby>」<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>和家</rb><rt>ʽo-kô</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>相信了</rb><rt>siang-sing-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>54</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>歸</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做出</rb><rt>tso-cʽih</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>第二個</rb><rt>di-nyi-go</rt><rb>神蹟</rb><rt>jing-tsih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。

---

### Iʽe. V.

<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>KEH-SING</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>節肯</rb><rt>tsih-kʽeng</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>羊門</rb><rt>Yiang-*meng*</rt><rb>相近</rb><rt>siang-gying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>池</rb><rt>dzi</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>希伯來</rb><rt>Hyi-pah-læ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>畢土大</rb><rt>Pih-tʽu-da</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>團圈</rb><rt>*dön-kyʽün*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>五個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>涼亭</rb><rt>liang-ding</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>裏向</rb><rt>Li-hyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一大潮</rb><rt>ih-do-dziao</rt><rb>病人</rb><rt>bing-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>瞎子</rb><rt>hah-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拐腳</rb><rt>kwa-kyiah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>瘋癱</rb><rt>fong-tʽæn</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>睏間</rb><rt>kwʽeng-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>單等</rb><rt>tæn-teng</rt><rb>池裏個</rb><rt>dzi-li-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>動起來</rb><rt>dong-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>有得</rb><rt>yiu-teh</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>降落</rb><rt>kông-lôh</rt><rb>池裏</rb><rt>dzi-li</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>動</rb><rt>dong</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>動好</rb><rt>dong-hao</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>跳落去</rb><rt>tʽiao-lôh-kyʽi</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗論</rb><rt>feh-leng</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>痊癒了</rb><rt>djün-yü-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Y. ʽE. 31. 耶（穌）後（頭）31（年）。（原註）</sup>

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>Keh-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>生勒</rb><rt>sang-leh</rt><rb>三十</rb><rt>sæn-jih</rt><rb>八</rb><rt>pah</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>睏間</rb><rt>kwʽeng-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>*bing*</rt><rb>生勒</rb><rt>sang-leh</rt><rb>長久了</rb><rt>dziang-kyiu-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>痊癒</rb><rt>djün-yü</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>病人</rb><rt>Bing-nying</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>動個</rb><rt>dong-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>嘸人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>擋</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt><rb>池裏</rb><rt>dzi-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我自</rb><rt>ngô-zi</rt><rb>去個</rb><rt>kyʽi-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>別人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>跳落去</rb><rt>tʽiao-lôh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>8</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爬起來</rb><rt>Bô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>鋪蓋</rb><rt>pʽu-kæ</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt></ruby>。」<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>痊癒</rb><rt>djün-yü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>鋪蓋</rb><rt>pʽu-kæ</rt><rb>走了</rb><rt>tseo-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>醫好個</rb><rt>i-hao-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>今末</rb><rt>*Kyih-mih*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>應該</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>鋪蓋</rb><rt>pʽu-kæ</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>Peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>痊癒</rb><rt>djün-yü</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>Do-leh</rt><rb>鋪蓋</rb><rt>pʽu-kæ</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt></ruby>。』」<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>謳</rb><rt>Eo</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>鋪蓋</rb><rt>pʽu-kæ</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>？」<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>醫好個</rb><rt>I-hao-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>箇𡍲</rb><rt>keh-tʽah</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一潮</rb><rt>ih-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>再話</rb><rt>tsæ-wô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>暗藏</rb><rt>en-dzông</rt><rb>走過了</rb><rt>tseo-ko-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *Yiu-teh* *fæn*, ing-we Yiæ-su, we-leh keh-tʽah di-fông yiu ih-dziao nying læ-tong, z yi-kying bi-ko-de. 有得翻，因爲<u>耶穌</u>，爲了箇𡍲地方有一潮人來東，是已經避過了。（原註）</sup>

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>聖殿裏</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt><rb>碰見</rb><rt>pʽong-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>痊癒了</rb><rt>djün-yü-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>犯罪</rb><rt>væn-ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>遭難</rb><rt>tsao-næn</rt><rb>越發</rb><rt>yüih-fah</rt><rb>厲害</rb><rt>li-ʽæ</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>Peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>痊癒</rb><rt>djün-yü</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>。」<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>逼難</rb><rt>pih-næn</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>打算</rb><rt>tang-sön</rt><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>如今</rb><rt>jü-kying</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。」<sup>18</sup> <sup>19</sup> <sup>24</sup> 「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真真</rb><rt>tsing-tsing</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>Tʽing-meng</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>*keh-go*</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>*nying*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>永遠</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>定罪</rb><rt>ding-ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>死個</rb><rt>si-go</rt><rb>地步</rb><rt>*di-bu*</rt><rb>趲過</rb><rt>tsæn-ko</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <sup>30</sup> 「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>主意</rb><rt>cü-i</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>聽聞個</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-go</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>判斷</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>判斷</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>想</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>Zi-go</rt><rb>主意</rb><rt>cü-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>想</rb><rt>*siang*</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>*i*</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>阿爹個</rb><rt>Ah-tia-go</rt><rb>主意</rb><rt>cü-i</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>單只</rb><rt>*tæn-tsih*</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>Zi-go</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>實惠個</rb><rt>jih-we-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>一位</rb><rt>Ih-we</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>實惠個</rb><rt>jih-we-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>存</rb><rt>dzeng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>差來個</rb><rt>tsʽa-læ-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。

<sup>39</sup> 「<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>考究</rb><rt>kʽao-kyiu</rt><rb>聖書</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>聖書裏</rb><rt>Sing-shü-li</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>永遠</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>聖書</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>Tsih-z</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>41</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>稱讚</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>42</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>愛敬</rb><rt>æ-kying</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt></ruby>。<sup>43</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>在於</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>阿爹個</rb><rt>Ah-tia-go</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>別人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt><rb>在於</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>反轉</rb><rt>*fæn-cün*</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>44</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>爾我個</rb><rt>ng-ngô-go</rt><rb>稱讚</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>想到</rb><rt>siang-tao</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>獨一位</rb><rt>doh-ih-we</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>稱讚</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<sup>45</sup> <ruby><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>M-nao</rt><rb>忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>狀</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>仰望個</rb><rt>nyiang-vông-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>46</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>總</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>寫的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>指點</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<sup>47</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>書</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」

### Iʽe. VI.

<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>KEH-SING</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>渡過</rb><rt>du-ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>*ziu-z*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>提比哩亞</rb><rt>Di-pi-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一大潮</rb><rt>ih-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>跟着</rb><rt>keng-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>病人個</rb><rt>bing-nying-go</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>神蹟</rb><rt>jing-tsih</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt><rb>山裏</rb><rt>sæn-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>坐落</rb><rt>zo-lôh</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>*Keh-go*</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>*z-ʽeo*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>逾越</rb><rt>Yü-yüih</rt><rb>節肯</rb><rt>tsih-kʽeng</rt><rb>相近了</rb><rt>siang-gying-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Y. ʽE. 32. 耶（穌）後（頭）32（年）。（原註）</sup>

<sup>5</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>抬起</rb><rt>dæ-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>一大潮</rb><rt>ih-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>腓力</rb><rt>Fi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽAh-li</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>買</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>喫食</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-zih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」（<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇句</rb><rt>keh-kyü</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>試試</rb><rt>s-s</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其自</rb><rt>Gyi-zi</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>打賬</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。）<sup>7</sup> <u><ruby><rb>腓力</rb><rt>Fi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>買</rb><rt>*Ma*</rt><rb>廿兩</rb><rt>nyiæn-liang</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>饅頭</rb><rt>mun-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>夠</rb><rt>keo</rt></ruby>。」<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>一個</rb><rt>Ih-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西門</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u><u>·</u><ruby><rb><u>彼得</u>個</rb><rt>Pe-teh-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>安得烈</rb><rt>En-teh-lih</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<sup>9</sup> 「<ruby><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>Dông-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>五個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>大麥</rb><rt>do-mah</rt><rb>饅頭</rb><rt>mun-deo</rt><rb>兩梗</rb><rt>liang-kwang</rt><rb>魚</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>*ih-ngæn*</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>介星</rb><rt>ka-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>Peh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>坐帶落</rb><rt>zo-tæn-lôh</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>青草</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsʽao</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>男</rb><rt>nen</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>坐落</rb><rt>zo-lôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>約來</rb><rt>iah-læ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>五千</rb><rt>ng-tsʽin</rt><rb>數目</rb><rt>su-moh</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>饅頭</rb><rt>mun-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>祝謝仔</rb><rt>coh-zia-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>交付</rb><rt>kao-fu</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>坐間個</rb><rt>zo-kæn-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>魚</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>*kyʽüoh*</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>已經</rb><rt>Yi-kying</rt><rb>喫飽了</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-pao-de</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>喫剩個</rb><rt>Kyʽüoh-dzing-go</rt><rb>零碎</rb><rt>ling-se</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>收拾攏</rb><rt>siu-jih-long</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>糟蹋掉</rb><rt>tsao-tʽah-diao</rt></ruby>。」<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>收拾攏</rb><rt>siu-jih-long</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>*cong-nying*</rt><rb>喫剩個</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-dzing-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>五個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>大麥</rb><rt>do-mah</rt><rb>饅頭</rb><rt>mun-deo</rt><rb>零碎</rb><rt>ling-se</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>𣥼滿</rb><rt>tsi-mun</rt><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>籃</rb><rt>læn</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>神蹟</rb><rt>jing-tsih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>實在</rb><rt>jih-dzæ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>當該</rb><rt>tông-kæ</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>Sin-cü-nying</rt></ruby>。」<sup>15</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>硬抲</rb><rt>ngang-kʽô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>獨自</rb><rt>doh-zi</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>山裏</rb><rt>sæn-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>夜快</rb><rt>Yia-kwʽa</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>邊沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt></ruby>，<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>跳落</rb><rt>tʽiao-lôh</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>渡過</rb><rt>du-ko</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦百農</rb><rt>Kyüô-pah-nong</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>已經</rb><rt>Yi-kying</rt><rb>暗了</rb><rt>en-de</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>大風</rb><rt>do-fong</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>發起來</rb><rt>fah-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>海裏</rb><rt>hæ-li</rt><rb>作浪</rb><rt>tsôh-lông</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>搖勒</rb><rt>yiao-leh</rt><rb>十頭八里</rb><rt>jih-deo-pah-li</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>面頂</rb><rt>min-teng</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>近攏</rb><rt>gying-long</rt><rb>船裏</rb><rt>jün-li</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>怕起來了</rb><rt>pʽô-kyʽi-læ-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>接</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>DI-NYI</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>立勒</rb><rt>lih-leh</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>對岸</rb><rt>te-ngen</rt><rb>箇潮</rb><rt>keh-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>別隻</rb><rt>bih-tsah</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>跳落個</rb><rt>tʽiao-lôh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>*hyiao-teh*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>去個</rb><rt>kyʽi-go</rt></ruby>，<sup>23</sup> （<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>幾隻</rb><rt>kyi-tsah</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>提比哩亞</rb><rt>Di-pi-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-leh</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>祝謝好</rb><rt>coh-zia-hao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>饅頭</rb><rt>mun-deo</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>相近</rb><rt>siang-gying</rt></ruby>。）<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>箇潮</rb><rt>keh-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>跳落</rb><rt>tʽiao-lôh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦百農</rb><rt>Kyüô-pah-nong</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>尋</rb><rt>zing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>對岸</rb><rt>te-ngen</rt><rb>尋着了</rb><rt>zing-djôh-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>夫子</rb><rt>Fu-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>幾時</rb><rt>kyi-z</rt><rb>到個</rb><rt>tao-go</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>26</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真真</rb><rt>tsing-tsing</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尋</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>神蹟</rb><rt>jing-tsih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>饅頭</rb><rt>mun-deo</rt><rb>喫飽</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-pao</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>緣故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>M-nao</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>饐掉</rb><rt>i-diao</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>喫食</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-zih</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>永遠</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt><rb>來東個</rb><rt>læ-tong-go</rt><rb>喫食</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-zih</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>*tso*</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>*kong-fu*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>天父</rb><rt>*Tʽin*-Vu</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>一顆</rb><rt>ih-kʽo</rt><rb>印</rb><rt>ing</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>印</rb><rt>ing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到底</rb><rt>tao-ti</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>啥西</rb><rt>soh-si</rt></ruby>？」<sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>Kyʽi</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>差來個</rb><rt>tsʽa-læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>神蹟</rb><rt>jing-tsih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>到底</rb><rt>tao-ti</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>？<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>嗎嗱</rb><rt>mô-nô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>書裏</rb><rt>*Shü-li*</rt><rb>寫的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>餅</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。』 」<sup>32</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真真</rb><rt>tsing-tsing</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>Dzong</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>餅</rb><rt>ping</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>並</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>現在</rb><rt>*yin-dzæ*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>真個</rb><rt>tsing-go</rt><rb>餅</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>餅</rb><rt>ping</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>降落來</rb><rt>kông-lôh-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>常常</rb><rt>djông-djông</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>餅</rb><rt>ping</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>35</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>活命個</rb><rt>weh-ming-go</rt><rb>餅</rb><rt>ping</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>永</rb><rt>üong</rt><rb>𣍐</rb><rt>ve</rt><rb>肚飢</rb><rt>du-kyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>永</rb><rt>üong</rt><rb>𣍐</rb><rt>ve</rt><rb>口渴</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeh</rt></ruby>。<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>*z-ka*</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>過了</rb><rt>ko-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>仍舊</rb><rt>*dzing-gyiu*</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>趕</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby>。<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>降落來</rb><rt>kông-lôh-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>Zi-go</rt><rb>主意</rb><rt>cü-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>*læ*</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>*tso*</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>主顧個</rb><rt>Cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>主意</rb><rt>cü-i</rt></ruby>。<sup>39</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>阿爹個</rb><rt>Ah-tia-go</rt><rb>主意</rb><rt>cü-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>錯落</rb><rt>tsʽo-lôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>末個</rb><rt>mah-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>爬起來</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>Tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>主顧個</rb><rt>Cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>主意</rb><rt>cü-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>永遠</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>末個</rb><rt>mah-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>爬起來</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>41</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>降落來個</rb><rt>kông-lôh-læ-go</rt><rb>餅</rb><rt>ping</rt></ruby>，」<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>私下</rb><rt>s-ʽô</rt><rb>媢怨</rb><rt>mao-ün</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<sup>42</sup> 「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>豈</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>約瑟</u>個</rb><rt>Iah-seh-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>？<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>爹孃</rb><rt>tia-nyiang</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>難道</rb><rt>*næn-dao*</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得個</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>降落來</rb><rt>kông-lôh-læ</rt></ruby>？』」

<ruby><rb>先知人個</rb><rt>Sin-cü-nying-go</rt><rb>書裏</rb><rt>*shü-li*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>寫的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>教訓</rb><rt>kao-hyüing</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>阿爹個</rb><rt>Ah-tia-go</rt><rb>教訓</rb><rt>kao-hyüing</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>46</sup> <ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>Yia</rt><rb>並</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>過</rb><rt>ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>出於</rb><rt>cʽih-ü</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>過了</rb><rt>ko-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>47</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>餅</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>降落來</rb><rt>kông-lôh-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>。<sup>51</sup> <sup>53</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真真</rb><rt>tsing-tsing</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>肉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>*sing*</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>總</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt></ruby>。<sup>54</sup> <sup>59</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦百農</rb><rt>Kyüô-pah-nong</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>聚會堂裏</rb><rt>jü-we-dông-li</rt><rb>教訓</rb><rt>kao-hyüing</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>60</sup> <ruby><rb>門徒</rb><rt>Meng-du</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>難勒緊</rb><rt>næn-leh-kying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」<sup>61</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>箇句</rb><rt>keh-kyü</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>私下</rb><rt>s-ʽô</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>媢怨</rb><rt>mao-ün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇句</rb><rt>Keh-kyü</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>𢲔跌</rb><rt>pæn-tih</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>62</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>升上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>來間個</rb><rt>læ-kæn-go</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>*dza*</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>*ni*</rt></ruby>？<sup>63</sup> <ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>S-teh</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>*nying*</rt><rb>活</rb><rt>weh</rt><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>靈</rb><rt>ling</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>肉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>無益</rb><rt>vu-ih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>靈</rb><rt>ling</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>64</sup> <ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信個</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>起頭</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信個</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>賣</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>掉</rb><rt>diao</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>。<sup>65</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>話過</rb><rt>wô-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>66</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>倒走轉</rb><rt>tao-tseo-cün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>跟着</rb><rt>keng-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>67</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>十二個</rb><rt>jih-nyi-go</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<sup>68</sup> <u><ruby><rb>西門</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u><u>·</u><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>誰個</rb><rt>jü-go</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>永遠</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>活命個</rb><rt>weh-ming-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>69</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>活個</rb><rt>weh-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>。」<sup>70</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>十二個</rb><rt>jih-nyi-go</rt><rb>豈</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>挑選個</rb><rt>tʽiao-shün-go</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>惡鬼</rb><rt>ôh-kyü</rt></ruby>。」<sup>71</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>指點</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加略</rb><rt>Kyüô-liah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>西門</u>個</rb><rt>Si-meng-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>十二個</rb><rt>jih-nyi-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>在內</rb><rt>dzæ-nen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>賣</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>掉</rb><rt>diao</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。
