### Iʽe. VII.

<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>KEH-SING</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走來走去</rb><rt>tseo-læ-tseo-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>要想</rb><rt>iao-siang</rt><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>2</sup> <u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>搭</rb><rt>tah</rt><rb>帳篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>節肯</rb><rt>tsih-kʽeng</rt><rb>相近了</rb><rt>siang-gying-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb><u>耶穌</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>要想</rb><rt>iao-siang</rt><rb>明當當</rb><rt>ming-tông-tông</rt><rb>顯出來</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>再𣍐</rb><rt>tsæ-ve</rt><rb>暗洞洞</rb><rt>en-dong-dong</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>顯出來</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。」<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>其自個</rb><rt>Gyi-zi-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>常常</rb><rt>djông-djông</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>世間上</rb><rt>Shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>惡愫</rb><rt>u-su</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>惡愫</rb><rt>u-su</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>行爲</rb><rt>ʽang-we</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>壞個</rb><rt>w̆a-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>上去</rb><rt>zông-kyʽi</rt><rb>守</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>節肯</rb><rt>tsih-kʽeng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>守</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>節肯</rb><rt>tsih-kʽeng</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>未了</rb><rt>mi-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>9</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>講好</rb><rt>kông-hao</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>仍舊</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>上去</rb><rt>zông-kyʽi</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>上去</rb><rt>zông-kyʽi</rt><rb>守</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>節肯</rb><rt>tsih-kʽeng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>明當當</rb><rt>ming-tông-tông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>暗洞洞</rb><rt>en-dong-dong</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>節肯</rb><rt>Tsih-kʽeng</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>尋</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt></ruby>？」<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>Pah-sing</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>大樣</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>議論紛紛</rb><rt>nyi-leng-feng-feng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>好人</rb><rt>hao-nying</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>Feh-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>帶掉</rb><rt>ta-diao</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>明當當</rb><rt>ming-tông-tông</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>節肯個</rb><rt>tsih-kʽeng-go</rt><rb>拗半</rb><rt>ao-pun</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt><rb>聖殿裏</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>書</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>讀過</rb><rt>doh-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>認得</rb><rt>nying-teh</rt><rb>字</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, yiu ʽôh-veng. 或話，有學問。（原註）</sup>

<sup>16</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>我自個</rb><rt>Ngô-zi-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>主顧個</rb><rt>Cü-kwu-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>注心</rb><rt>cü-sing</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>旨意</rb><rt>ts-i</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>還是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>出於</rb><rt>cʽih-ü</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>我自</rb><rt>Ngô-zi</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>主意</rb><rt>cü-i</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>主意</rb><rt>cü-i</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>想</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>想</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>主顧個</rb><rt>cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>實惠個</rb><rt>jih-we-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>偏曲心</rb><rt>pʽin-kyʽüoh-sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>律法</rb><rt>Lih-fah</rt><rb>難道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>嘸人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>弗然</rb><rt>*feh-jün*</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>打算</rb><rt>tang-sön</rt><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>Pah-sing</rt><rb>應起</rb><rt>ing-kyʽi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>入魔的了</rb><rt>jih-mo-tih-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>打算</rb><rt>tang-sön</rt><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>？」<sup>21</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>做過</rb><rt>tso-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>周割禮</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，（<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>*keh*</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt><rb>起頭</rb><rt>*kyʽi-deo*</rt></ruby>，）<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>周割禮</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>周割禮</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>省勒</rb><rt>sæn-leh</rt><rb>違背</rb><rt>we-be</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>摩西</u>個</rb><rt>Mo-si-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>渾身</rb><rt>weng-sing</rt><rb>痊癒</rb><rt>djün-yü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>恨</rb><rt>ʽeng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>M-nao</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>外光面</rb><rt>nga-kwông-min</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>判斷</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>正直個</rb><rt>tsing-dzih-go</rt><rb>判斷</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>判斷</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>打算</rb><rt>tang-sön</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>弄煞個</rb><rt>long-sah-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>明當當</rb><rt>ming-tông-tông</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>官府</rb><rt>kwun-fu</rt><rb>難道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>實爲</rb><rt>jih-we</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>真真</rb><rt>tsing-tsing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>？<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>來歷</rb><rt>læ-lih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>來歷</rb><rt>læ-lih</rt><rb>嘸人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>28</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>聖殿裏</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>響響</rb><rt>hyiang-hyiang</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>來歷</rb><rt>læ-lih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>主意</rb><rt>cü-i</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>真個</rb><rt>tsing-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。」<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>打算</rb><rt>tang-sön</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>嘸人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>下手</rb><rt>ʽô-siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>Pah-sing</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>難道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>神蹟</rb><rt>jing-tsih</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>多如</rb><rt>to-jü</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>32</sup> <u><ruby><rb>法利賽</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>議論</rb><rt>nyi-leng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>打發</rb><rt>tang-fah</rt><rb>差人</rb><rt>tsʽa-nying</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>33</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>一晌</rb><rt>ih-zông</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>尋</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>尋弗着</rb><rt>zing-feh-djôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>來間個</rb><rt>læ-kæn-go</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby>。」<sup>35</sup> <u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>打賬</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>尋</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>弗着</rb><rt>feh-djôh</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>難道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>散開</rb><rt>sæn-kʽæ</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希臘</rb><rt>Hyi-lah</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>*pah-sing*</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>*iao*</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希臘</rb><rt>Hyi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>尋</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>尋弗着</rb><rt>zing-feh-djôh</rt></ruby>，』<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>來間個</rb><rt>læ-kæn-go</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>箇是</rb><rt>keh-z</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, bih-koh. 或話，別國。（原註）</sup>

<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt><rb>節肯</rb><rt>tsih-kʽeng</rt><rb>結煞</rb><rt>kyih-sah</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>*nyih-ts*</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>立仔</rb><rt>lih-ts</rt><rb>響響</rb><rt>hyiang-hyiang</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>口渴</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt></ruby>。<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>相信</rb><rt>Siang-sing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>聖書</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>Dzong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>肚皮</rb><rt>du-bi</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>流出</rb><rt>liu-cʽih</rt><rb>活水</rb><rt>weh-shü</rt><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。」<sup>39</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇句</rb><rt>keh-kyü</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>指點</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>賜落來</rb><rt>s-*lôh-læ*</rt></ruby>。

<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>Pah-sing</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>實爲</rb><rt>jih-we</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>Sin-cü-nying</rt></ruby>。」<sup>41</sup> <ruby><rb>有得個</rb><rt>Yiu-teh-go</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>。」<ruby><rb>有得個</rb><rt>Yiu-teh-go</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>難道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>42</sup> <ruby><rb>聖書裏</rb><rt>Sing-shü-li</rt><rb>豈</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>大闢</u>個</rb><rt>Da-bih-go</rt><rb>子孫</rb><rt>Ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯利恆</rb><rt>Pah-li-ʽeng</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>大闢</rb><rt>Da-bih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>庉個</rb><rt>deng-go</rt><rb>鄉村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt></ruby>？」<sup>43</sup> <ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>Pah-sing</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>爭論</rb><rt>tsang-leng</rt></ruby>。<sup>44</sup> <ruby><rb>內中</rb><rt>Nen-cong</rt><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>嘸人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>下手</rb><rt>ʽô-siu</rt></ruby>。

<sup>45</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>差人</rb><rt>tsʽa-nying</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利賽</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>？」<sup>46</sup> <ruby><rb>差人</rb><rt>Tsʽa-nying</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>*gyi-lah*</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>Dzong</rt><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。」<sup>47</sup> <u><ruby><rb>法利賽</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>帶掉了</rb><rt>ta-diao-de</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>48</sup> <ruby><rb>官府</rb><rt>Kwun-fu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利賽</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>49</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>咒罵個</rb><rt>tsiu-mô-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>50</sup> <u><ruby><rb>尼哥底母</rb><rt>Nyi-ko-ti-meo</rt></ruby></u>，（<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一夜</rb><rt>ih-yia</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>內</rb><rt>nen</rt></ruby>，）<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<sup>51</sup> 「<ruby><rb>阿拉個</rb><rt>Ah-lah-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>口供</rb><rt>kʽeo-kong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>先</rb><rt>*sin*</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>難道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>定</rb><rt>ding</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<sup>52</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>難道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>查查看</rb><rt>dzô-dzô-kʽen</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>53</sup> <ruby><rb>各人</rb><rt>Kôh-nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。

---

### Iʽe. VIII.

<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>YIÆ-SU</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>橄欖</rb><rt>Ken-læn</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>早</rb><rt>Tsao</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>聖殿裏</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>坐落</rb><rt>zo-lôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>讀書人</rb><rt>Doh-shü-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利賽</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>姦淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>嫁出個</rb><rt>kô-cʽih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<sup>4</sup> 「<ruby><rb>先生</rb><rt>Sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>姦淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>當時</rb><rt>tông-z</rt><rb>抲着個</rb><rt>kʽô-djôh-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>箇株介</rb><rt>Keh-cü-ka</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>律法裏</rb><rt>lih-fah-li</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>揞煞個</rb><rt>kʽang-sah-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇句</rb><rt>keh-kyü</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>試試</rb><rt>s-s</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>*ih-go*</rt><rb>因頭</rb><rt>*ing-deo*</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby>。

<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>傴倒</rb><rt>eo-tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>指頭</rb><rt>ts-deo</rt><rb>地垟裏</rb><rt>di-yiang-li</rt><rb>且顧</rb><rt>*tsʽia-kwu*</rt><rb>寫</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>字</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>歇口</rb><rt>hyih-kʽeo</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>直起來</rb><rt>dzih-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>嘸罪個</rb><rt>m-ze-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>一塊</rb><rt>ih-kwʽe</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>*kyʽi*</rt><rb>揞</rb><rt>kʽang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>傴倒</rb><rt>eo-tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>地垟裏</rb><rt>di-yiang-li</rt><rb>寫</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>字</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>良心</rb><rt>liang-sing</rt><rb>責罰</rb><rt>tsah-vah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>老老少少</rb><rt>lao-lao-siao-siao</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>走出了</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>剩了</rb><rt>dzing-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>立東個</rb><rt>lih-tong-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>直起來</rb><rt>dzih-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>Nyü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>嘸人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>定</rb><rt>ding</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>定</rb><rt>ding</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>犯罪</rb><rt>væn-ze</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>12</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>YIÆ-SU</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>世間上個</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông-go</rt><rb>亮光</rb><rt>Liang-kwông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>跟着</rb><rt>keng-djôh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>暗路</rb><rt>en-lu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>活命個</rb><rt>weh-ming-go</rt><rb>亮光</rb><rt>liang-kwông</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>法利賽</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾自</rb><rt>Ng-zi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>Zi-go</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>*sô-yi*</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>真個</rb><rt>tsing-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>14</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>Ziu-z</rt><rb>我自</rb><rt>Ngô-zi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>Zi-go</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>真個</rb><rt>tsing-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我自</rb><rt>Ngô-zi</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>判斷</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>判斷</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>判斷</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>判斷</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>真個</rb><rt>tsing-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>獨自</rb><rt>doh-zi</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>寫的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>兩個</rb><rt>Liang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>真個</rb><rt>tsing-go</rt></ruby>。 <sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>我自</rb><rt>Ngô-zi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>Zi-go</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt></ruby>？」<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>*hyiao-teh*</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>聖殿</rb><rt>sing-din</rt><rb>錢庫</rb><rt>dzin-kwʽu</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>嘸人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>21</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>去仔</rb><rt>kyʽi-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>尋</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>罪孽</rb><rt>ze-nyih</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>去個</rb><rt>kyʽi-go</rt><rb>烏蕩</rb><rt>u-dông</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby>。」<sup>22</sup> <u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>去個</rb><rt>kyʽi-go</rt><rb>烏蕩</rb><rt>u-dông</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby>，』<ruby><rb>要防</rb><rt>iao-bông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>尋</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>。」<sup>23</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>屬於</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt><rb>下頭</rb><rt>ʽô-deo</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>屬於</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt><rb>上頭</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>屬於</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>屬於</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>話過</rb><rt>wô-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>罪孽</rb><rt>ze-nyih</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>*jü*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>罪孽</rb><rt>ze-nyih</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>？」<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>一向</rb><rt>ih-hyiang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>話個</rb><rt>wô-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗然</rb><rt>*feh-jün*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>關着</rb><rt>kwæn-djôh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>批評</rb><rt>pʽi-bing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>實惠個</rb><rt>jih-we-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>聽聞個</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>講出來</rb><rt>kông-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。」<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>指點</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby>。

<sup>28</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>提上去</rb><rt>di-zông-kyʽi</rt><rb>之後</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>*jü*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>*yia*</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>*we*</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>*hyiao-teh*</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>主意</rb><rt>cü-i</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>教訓</rb><rt>kao-hyüing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>Tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>獨自</rb><rt>doh-zi</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>常常</rb><rt>djông-djông</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>歡喜個</rb><rt>hwun-hyi-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>31</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>常常</rb><rt>djông-djông</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>實爲</rb><rt>jih-we</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>；<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>釋放</rb><rt>sih-fông</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亞伯拉罕</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen-go</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>誰個</rb><rt>jü-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>釋放了個</rb><rt>sih-fông-liao-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>？」<sup>34</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真真</rb><rt>tsing-tsing</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>犯罪個</rb><rt>væn-ze-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>罪個</rb><rt>ze-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>。<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>Nu-boh</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>永遠</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>永遠</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>庉東</rb><rt>deng-tong</rt></ruby>。<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>釋放</rb><rt>sih-fông</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>實在</rb><rt>jih-dzæ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>釋放了個</rb><rt>sih-fông-liao-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亞伯拉罕</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen-go</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>要想</rb><rt>iao-siang</rt><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>嘸陶成</rb><rt>m-dao-dzing</rt></ruby>。<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>看見過</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>看見過</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>39</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉個</rb><rt>Ah-lah-go</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u>。」<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亞伯拉罕</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen-go</rt><rb>兒囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>總</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亞伯拉罕</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen-go</rt><rb>行爲</rb><rt>ʽang-we</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>要想</rb><rt>iao-siang</rt><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>聽來個</rb><rt>tʽing-læ-go</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>並</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做法</rb><rt>tso-fah</rt></ruby>。<sup>41</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>阿爹個</rb><rt>ah-tia-go</rt><rb>行爲</rb><rt>ʽang-we</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。」

<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>姦淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>生個</rb><rt>sang-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>42</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>總</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>愛敬</rb><rt>æ-kying</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>出於</rb><rt>cʽih-ü</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>*tao*</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>*shü-kæn-zông*</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>主意</rb><rt>cü-i</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>43</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>緣故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt></ruby>。<sup>44</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>屬於</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt><rb>魔鬼</rb><rt>mo-kwe</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>*ziu-z*</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>阿爹個</rb><rt>ah-tia-go</rt><rb>私慾</rb><rt>s-yüôh</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>起頭</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>殺人個</rb><rt>sah-nying-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>保守</rb><rt>pao-siu</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>存</rb><rt>dzeng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>謊話</rb><rt>hwông-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>說謊個</rb><rt>shih-hwông-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>說謊</rb><rt>shih-hwông</rt><rb>主顧個</rb><rt>cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt></ruby>。<sup>45</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>真話</rb><rt>tsing-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<sup>46</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>Ng-lah-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>靠實</rb><rt>kʽao-jih</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>真話</rb><rt>tsing-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>？<sup>47</sup> <ruby><rb>屬於</rb><rt>Joh-ü</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>屬於</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>48</sup> <u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>應起</rb><rt>ing-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒瑪利亞</rb><rt>Sah-mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>入魔個</rb><rt>jih-mo-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>豈</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>真個</rb><rt>tsing-go</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<sup>49</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>入魔個</rb><rt>jih-mo-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>敬重</rb><rt>kying-djong</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>敬重</rb><rt>kying-djong</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<sup>50</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>打算</rb><rt>tang-sön</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>Zi-go</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>打算</rb><rt>tang-sön</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>*Ngô*</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>*yüong-wô*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>申</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>*Ngô*</rt><rb>冤</rb><rt>ün</rt></ruby>。<sup>51</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真真</rb><rt>tsing-tsing</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>保守</rb><rt>pao-siu</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>永遠</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>52</sup> <u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>入了魔了</rb><rt>jih-leh-mo-de</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>*si-de*</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>保守</rb><rt>pao-siu</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>永遠</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>死個</rb><rt>si-go</rt><rb>苦頭</rb><rt>*kwʽu-deo*</rt></ruby>。』<sup>53</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>難道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>大如</rb><rt>do-jü</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>54</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>稱讚</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsæn</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>稱讚</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsæn</rt><rb>算弗得</rb><rt>sön-feh-teh</rt><rb>稱讚</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsæn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>稱讚</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsæn</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>55</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>認得</rb><rt>nying-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>說謊</rb><rt>shih-hwông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>保守</rb><rt>pao-siu</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>56</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>Ng-lah-go</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>*hyiao-teh*</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>高興</rb><rt>kao-hying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>看見過</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>歡喜了</rb><rt>hwun-hyi-de</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>57</sup> <u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>年紀</rb><rt>nyin-kyi</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>五十</rb><rt>ng-jih</rt><rb>歲</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>難道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>過了</rb><rt>ko-de</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<sup>58</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真真</rb><rt>tsing-tsing</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>生出</rb><rt>sang-cʽih</rt><rb>以前</rb><rt>yi-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>來東個</rb><rt>læ-tong-go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>59</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>揞</rb><rt>kʽang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>避過</rb><rt>bi-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>聖殿</rb><rt>sing-din</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是介</rb><rt>ziu-z-ka</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。

### Iʽe. IX.

<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*YIÆ-SU*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去個</rb><rt>kyʽi-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>生出</rb><rt>sang-cʽih</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>瞎眼個</rb><rt>hah-ngæn-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>夫子</rb><rt>Fu-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>生出</rb><rt>sang-cʽih</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>瞎眼</rb><rt>hah-ngæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>誰個</rb><rt>jü-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>還是</rb><rt>Wa-z</rt><rb>其自個</rb><rt>gyi-zi-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>大人個</rb><rt>do-nying-go</rt></ruby>？」<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>*gyi-lah*</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>Yia</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>其自個</rb><rt>gyi-zi-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>大人個</rb><rt>do-nying-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>*ze*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>顯出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>現在</rb><rt>*Yin-dzæ*</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>日裏</rb><rt>nyih-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>主顧個</rb><rt>Cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>夜到</rb><rt>yia-tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>弗來</rb><rt>feh-læ</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>快</rb><rt>*kwʽa*</rt><rb>來了</rb><rt>læ-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>世間上個</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông-go</rt><rb>亮光</rb><rt>Liang-kwông</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>講好</rb><rt>kông-hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>吐</rb><rt>tʽu</rt><rb>饞唾</rb><rt>zæn-tʽu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>地垟</rb><rt>di-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>饞唾</rb><rt>zæn-tʽu</rt><rb>調</rb><rt>diao</rt><rb>爛泥</rb><rt>na-nyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>爛泥</rb><rt>na-nyi</rt><rb>搽</rb><rt>djô</rt><rb>瞎子個</rb><rt>hah-ts-go</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<sup>7</sup> 「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西羅亞</rb><rt>Si-lo-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>池裏</rb><rt>dzi-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>滰滰</rb><rt>gyiang-gyiang</rt></ruby>。」（<u><ruby><rb>西羅亞</rb><rt>Si-lo-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>「<ruby><rb>差來個</rb><rt>tsʽa-læ-go</rt></ruby>」<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>解說</rb><rt>ka-shih</rt></ruby>。）<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>滰</rb><rt>gyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>轉來</rb><rt>cün-læ</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>看見了</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>鄰舍家</rb><rt>Ling-sô-kô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>素常</rb><rt>su-djông</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>瞎眼</rb><rt>hah-ngæn</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>豈</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>坐勒</rb><rt>zo-leh</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>討飯</rb><rt>tʽao-væn</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>有得個</rb><rt>Yiu-teh-go</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>有得個</rb><rt>yiu-teh-go</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>相貌</rb><rt>Siang-mao</rt><rb>相像</rb><rt>siang-ziang</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其自</rb><rt>Gyi-zi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>開發個</rb><rt>kʽæ-fah-go</rt></ruby>？」<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>調勒</rb><rt>diao-leh</rt><rb>爛泥</rb><rt>na-nyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>搽</rb><rt>djô</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西羅亞</rb><rt>Si-lo-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>池裏</rb><rt>dzi-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>滰</rb><rt>gyiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>走去</rb><rt>tseo-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>滰</rb><rt>gyiang</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>看見了</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>以前</rb><rt>yi-zin</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>瞎眼</rb><rt>hah-ngæn</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>帶勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>見</rb><rt>kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利賽</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>調</rb><rt>diao</rt><rb>爛泥</rb><rt>na-nyi</rt><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利賽</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>看見個</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>爛泥</rb><rt>na-nyi</rt><rb>搽</rb><rt>djô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>滰滰</rb><rt>gyiang-gyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>*næn-kæn*</rt><rb>看見了</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利賽</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>有得個</rb><rt>yiu-teh-go</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>守</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>有得個</rb><rt>yiu-teh-go</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>罪人</rb><rt>Ze-nying</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>箇株介</rb><rt>keh-cü-ka</rt><rb>神蹟</rb><rt>jing-tsih</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>爭論</rb><rt>tsang-leng</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>瞎子</rb><rt>hah-ts</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>18</sup> <u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>瞎子</rb><rt>hah-ts</rt><rb>醫亮個</rb><rt>i-liang-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>大人</rb><rt>do-nying</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<sup>19</sup> 「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>生出</rb><rt>sang-cʽih</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>瞎眼</rb><rt>hah-ngæn</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>看見個</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-go</rt></ruby>？」<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>大人</rb><rt>do-nying</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>曉得個</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>生出</rb><rt>sang-cʽih</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>瞎眼</rb><rt>hah-ngæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>*ah-lah*</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>*yia*</rt><rb>曉得個</rb><rt>*hyiao-teh-go*</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>看見個</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-go</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>成人長大</rb><rt>dzing-nying-tsiang-da</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其自個</rb><rt>gyi-zi-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>話個</rb><rt>wô-go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>大人</rb><rt>do-nying</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>約好</rb><rt>iah-hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>如果</rb><rt>jü-ko</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>認着</rb><rt>nying-djôh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>趕出</rb><rt>ken-cʽih</rt><rb>聚會堂</rb><rt>jü-we-dông</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>大人</rb><rt>do-nying</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>成人長大</rb><rt>dzing-nying-tsiang-da</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>24</sup> <u><ruby><rb>法利賽</rb><rt>*Fah-li-sæ*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>*nying*</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>瞎眼</rb><rt>hah-ngæn</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>罪人</rb><rt>ze-nying</rt></ruby>。」<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>罪人</rb><rt>ze-nying</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>罪人</rb><rt>ze-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>瞎子</rb><rt>hah-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>曉得個</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>做啥個</rb><rt>tso-soh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>咋</rb><rt>Dza</rt><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt></ruby>？」<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>話過</rb><rt>wô-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>難道</rb><rt>Næn-dao</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>𧮙罵</rb><rt>zôh-mô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>摩西</u>個</rb><rt>Mo-si-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>曉得個</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>稀奇個</rb><rt>hyi-gyi-go</rt></ruby>！<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>罪人個</rb><rt>ze-nying-go</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>*tao-kao*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>虔心</rb><rt>gyin-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>旨意</rb><rt>ts-i</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>Dzong</rt><rb>開闢</rb><rt>kʽæ-bih</rt><rb>以來</rb><rt>yi-læ</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>生成</rb><rt>sang-dzing</rt><rb>瞎子個</rb><rt>hah-ts-go</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。」<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>應起</rb><rt>ing-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>和然</rb><rt>ʽo-jün</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>罪孽</rb><rt>ze-nyih</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>生出來個</rb><rt>sang-cʽih-læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>教訓</rb><rt>kao-hyüing</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>趕</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。
