<sup>35</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>YIÆ-SU</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>趕出了</rb><rt>ken-cʽih-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>尋着</rb><rt>zing-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>*Jing-ming-go*</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>*Ng-ts*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>？」<sup>37</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>。」<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>相信了</rb><rt>siang-sing-de</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>隨手</rb><rt>*Ze-siu*</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>39</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>判斷個</rb><rt>pʽun-tön-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>看見個</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見個</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>反轉</rb><rt>*fæn-cün*</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>瞎子</rb><rt>hah-ts</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>幾個</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利賽</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>旁邊</rb><rt>bông-pin</rt><rb>來東個</rb><rt>læ-tong-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>難道</rb><rt>Næn-dao</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>瞎子</rb><rt>hah-ts</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<sup>41</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>瞎子</rb><rt>hah-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>看見個</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>仍舊</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt></ruby>。」

### Iʽe. X.

「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真真</rb><rt>tsing-tsing</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>Feh</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>門</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>羊圈裏</rb><rt>yiang-gyin-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>別處</rb><rt>bih-cʽü</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>跳進去</rb><rt>tʽiao-tsing-kyʽi</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>賊人</rb><rt>zeh-nying</rt><rb>強盜</rb><rt>gyiang-dao</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>Dzong</rt><rb>門裏</rb><rt>meng-li</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>走進去</rb><rt>tseo-tsing-kyʽi</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>看羊個</rb><rt>kʽen-yiang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>管門個</rb><rt>Kwun-meng-go</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>門</rb><rt>*meng*</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>聽出</rb><rt>tʽing-cʽih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>聲音</rb><rt>sing-ing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>羊個</rb><rt>yiang-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>自個</rb><rt>Zi-go</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>放出仔</rb><rt>fông-cʽih-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>認得</rb><rt>nying-teh</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>聲音</rb><rt>sing-ing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>跟着</rb><rt>keng-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>跟着</rb><rt>keng-djôh</rt><rb>生頭</rb><rt>sang-deo</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>躲避</rb><rt>to-bi</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>認得</rb><rt>nying-teh</rt><rb>生頭</rb><rt>sang-deo</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>聲音</rb><rt>sing-ing</rt></ruby>。」<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真真</rb><rt>tsing-tsing</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>羊個</rb><rt>yiang-go</rt><rb>門</rb><rt>Meng</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>先如</rb><rt>sin-jü</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>賊人</rb><rt>zeh-nying</rt><rb>強盜</rb><rt>gyiang-dao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>門</rb><rt>Meng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>走進個</rb><rt>tseo-tsing-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>進進出出</rb><rt>tsing-tsing-cʽih-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>青草</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsʽao</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>賊</rb><rt>Zeh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>單只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>偷盜</rb><rt>tʽeo-dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>殺戮</rb><rt>sah-loh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>抄滅</rb><rt>tsʽao-mih</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>*yiang*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>活上</rb><rt>weh-zông</rt><rb>加</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>活</rb><rt>weh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>11</sup> 「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>看羊個</rb><rt>kʽen-yiang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>看羊個</rb><rt>kʽen-yiang-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>替</rb><rt>tʽi</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>捨出</rb><rt>sô-cʽih</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>Zi-go</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>Feh-z</rt><rb>看羊</rb><rt>kʽen-yiang</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>其自個</rb><rt>gyi-zi-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>僱來個</rb><rt>kwu-læ-go</rt><rb>傭人</rb><rt>yüong-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>豺狼</rb><rt>za-lông</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>棄掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>逃走</rb><rt>dao-tseo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>豺狼</rb><rt>za-lông</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>拖</rb><rt>tʽa</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>和潮</rb><rt>ʽo-dziao</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>趕散</rb><rt>ken-sæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>僱來個</rb><rt>Kwu-læ-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>逃走</rb><rt>dao-tseo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>僱來個</rb><rt>kwu-læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>顧着</rb><rt>kwu-djôh</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>看羊個</rb><rt>kʽen-yiang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>認得</rb><rt>nying-teh</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>Zi-go</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>*yiang*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>*yiang*</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>認得</rb><rt>nying-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<sup>15</sup> <sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>爭論</rb><rt>tsang-leng</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>內中</rb><rt>Nen-cong</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>附的了</rb><rt>vu-tih-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>發癲個</rb><rt>fah-tin-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>？」<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>有得個</rb><rt>Yiu-teh-go</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>附的</rb><rt>vu-tih</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>難道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>瞎子個</rb><rt>hah-ts-go</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>25</sup> <sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>揞</rb><rt>kʽang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>奉</rb><rt>vong</rt><rb>阿爹個</rb><rt>Ah-tia-go</rt><rb>號令</rb><rt>ʽao-ling</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>過</rb><rt>ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>揞</rb><rt>kʽang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>？」<sup>33</sup> <u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>揞</rb><rt>kʽang</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>褻瀆個</rb><rt>sih-doh-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。」<sup>34</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>書裏</rb><rt>*shü-li*</rt><rb>豈</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>寫的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>jing-ming</rt></ruby>？』 <sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>接着</rb><rt>tsih-djôh</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>號令</rb><rt>ʽao-ling</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>jing-ming</rt></ruby>，（<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>聖書個</rb><rt>Sing-shü-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>拆弗掉個</rb><rt>tsʽah-feh-diao-go</rt></ruby>，）<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>何況</rb><rt>*ʽo-hwông*</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>尊起</rb><rt>tseng-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>，』<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>褻瀆</rb><rt>sih-doh</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>阿爹個</rb><rt>Ah-tia-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>在於</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>在於</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>39</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>要想</rb><rt>iao-siang</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>脫出</rb><rt>tʽeh-cʽih</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河外</rb><rt>ʽo-nga</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt><rb>起頭</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>庉落</rb><rt>deng-lôh</rt></ruby>。<sup>41</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>神蹟</rb><rt>jing-tsih</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>做過</rb><rt>tso-ko</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>講到</rb><rt>kông-tao</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>句打句</rb><rt>kyü-tang-kyü</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>實惠個</rb><rt>jih-we-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>42</sup> <ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。

---

### Iʽe. XI.

<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>YIU</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>*ming-z*</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>*kyiao-leh*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉撒路</rb><rt>Lah-sah-lu</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯大尼</rb><rt>Pah-da-nyi</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>馬利亞</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>馬利亞</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>姊妹</rb><rt>tsi-me</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>馬大</u>個</rb><rt>Mô-da-go</rt><rb>鄉村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> （<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>Ziu-z</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>香油</rb><rt>hyiang-yiu</rt><rb>搽搽</rb><rt>djô-djô</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>頭髮</rb><rt>deo-fah</rt><rb>揩揩</rb><rt>kʽa-kʽa</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>腳</rb><rt>kyiah</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>馬利亞</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉撒路</rb><rt>Lah-sah-lu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>生病間</rb><rt>sang-bing-kæn</rt></ruby>。）<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>兩</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>姊妹</rb><rt>tsi-me</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>見</rb><rt>kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>愛惜個</rb><rt>æ-sih-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>4</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>甚至於</rb><rt>zing-ts-ü</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>5</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>愛惜</rb><rt>æ-sih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>馬大</rb><rt>Mô-da</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>姊妹</rb><rt>tsi-me</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉撒路</rb><rt>Lah-sah-lu</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>既然</rb><rt>Kyi-jün</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>庉上</rb><rt>deng-zông</rt><rb>兩</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>門徒</rb><rt>Meng-du</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>夫子</rb><rt>Fu-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>近來</rb><rt>gying-læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>要想</rb><rt>iao-siang</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>揞</rb><rt>kʽang</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<sup>9</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>日裏</rb><rt>Nyih-li</rt><rb>豈</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>點鐘</rb><rt>tin-cong</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>日裏</rb><rt>nyih-li</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>𢲔跌</rb><rt>pæn-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>世間上個</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông-go</rt><rb>亮光</rb><rt>liang-kwông</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>夜到</rb><rt>yia-tao</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>𢲔跌</rb><rt>pæn-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>亮光</rb><rt>liang-kwông</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>講好</rb><rt>kông-hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>朋友</rb><rt>beng-yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉撒路</rb><rt>Lah-sah-lu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>睏熟了</rb><rt>kwʽeng-joh-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>調覺</rb><rt>diao-kao</rt></ruby>。」<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>門徒</rb><rt>Meng-du</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>睏熟</rb><rt>kwʽeng-joh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>好其</rb><rt>hao-gyi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb><u>耶穌</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>指點</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>道是</rb><rt>dao-z</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>安安然然</rb><rt>en-en-jün-jün</rt><rb>睏熟間</rb><rt>kwʽeng-joh-kæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>明當當</rb><rt>ming-tông-tông</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>拉撒路</rb><rt>Lah-sah-lu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>緣故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>烏蕩</rb><rt>u-dông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>16</sup> <sup>17</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-leh</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>墳墓裏</rb><rt>veng-mo-li</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>四</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯大尼</rb><rt>Pah-da-nyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>相近</rb><rt>siang-gying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大約</rb><rt>da-iah</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽô</rt><rb>六里</rb><rt>loh-li</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>馬大</rb><rt>Mô-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>馬利亞</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>烏蕩</rb><rt>u-dông</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>兄弟個</rb><rt>hyüong-di-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>安慰</rb><rt>en-w̆e</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <u><ruby><rb>馬大</rb><rt>Mô-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來了</rb><rt>læ-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>迎接</rb><rt>nying-tsih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>馬利亞</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>仍舊</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>坐間</rb><rt>zo-kæn</rt></ruby>。

<sup>21</sup> <u><ruby><rb>馬大</rb><rt>Mô-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>𣍐</rb><rt>ve</rt><rb>死個</rb><rt>si-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>隨便</rb><rt>ze-bin</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>啥西</rb><rt>soh-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>。」<sup>23</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>爬起來</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>。」<sup>24</sup> <u><ruby><rb>馬大</rb><rt>Mô-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>末個</rb><rt>mah-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>活轉來</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>爬起來</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>。」<sup>25</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>Tsæ</rt><rb>爬起來</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>活個</rb><rt>weh-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>活東個</rb><rt>weh-tong-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>永遠</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>*shih-wô*</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<sup>27</sup> <u><ruby><rb>馬大</rb><rt>*Mô-da*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>當該</rb><rt>tông-kæ</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>講好</rb><rt>kông-hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>偷伴</rb><rt>tʽeo-bun</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>姊妹</rb><rt>tsi-me</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>馬利亞</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>先生</rb><rt>Sin-sang</rt><rb>來了</rb><rt>læ-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。」<sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>馬利亞</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>快快</rb><rt>kwʽa-kwʽa</rt><rb>立起來</rb><rt>lih-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>鄉村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>馬大</rb><rt>Mô-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>迎接</rb><rt>nying-tsih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>烏蕩</rb><rt>u-dông</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>馬利亞</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>安慰</rb><rt>en-w̆e</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>馬利亞</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>快快</rb><rt>kwʽa-kwʽa</rt><rb>立起來</rb><rt>lih-kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>走出去</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>keng-leh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>墳頭</rb><rt>veng-deo</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>哭</rb><rt>kʽoh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>32</sup> <u><ruby><rb>馬利亞</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>撲倒</rb><rt>pʽoh-tao</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>腳下</rb><rt>kyiah-ʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>𣍐</rb><rt>ve</rt><rb>死個</rb><rt>si-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>33</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>馬利亞</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>哭</rb><rt>kʽoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>同隊</rb><rt>dong-de</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>哭</rb><rt>kʽoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>傷心相</rb><rt>sông-sing-siang</rt><rb>難過</rb><rt>næn-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<sup>34</sup> 「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>安</rb><rt>en</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。」<sup>35</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>眼淚</rb><rt>ngæn-li</rt><rb>出了</rb><rt>cʽih-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>36</sup> <u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>且看</rb><rt>Tsʽia-kʽen</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>愛惜</rb><rt>æ-sih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>內中</rb><rt>Nen-cong</rt><rb>有得個</rb><rt>yiu-teh-go</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>瞎子個</rb><rt>hah-ts-go</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>難道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>38</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>傷心相</rb><rt>sông-sing-siang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>墳頭</rb><rt>veng-deo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>墳</rb><rt>*Veng*</rt><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>地穴</rb><rt>di-yüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一塊</rb><rt>ih-kwʽe</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>蓋間</rb><rt>kæ-kæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>39</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>石頭</rb><rt>Zah-deo</rt><rb>馱過仔</rb><rt>do-ko-ts</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>死個</rb><rt>Si-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>姊妹</rb><rt>tsi-me</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>馬大</rb><rt>Mô-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>氣味</rb><rt>kyʽi-mi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>四</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>40</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>難道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>話過</rb><rt>wô-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>？」<sup>41</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>馱過</rb><rt>do-ko</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>葬間</rb><rt>tsông-kæn</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>箇塊</rb><rt>keh-kwʽe</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt></ruby>。

<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>抬起</rb><rt>dæ-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>謝謝</rb><rt>zia-zia</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>。<sup>42</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>時常</rb><rt>z-djông</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>不過</rb><rt>peh-ko</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>團圈</rb><rt>dön-kyʽün</rt><rb>立東</rb><rt>lih-tong</rt><rb>箇潮</rb><rt>keh-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>43</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>講好</rb><rt>kông-hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>聲響</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>*z-ka*</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>拉撒路</rb><rt>Lah-sah-lu</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>走出來</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。」<sup>44</sup> <ruby><rb>死個</rb><rt>Si-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走出來</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>腳</rb><rt>kyiah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>麻布</rb><rt>mô-pu</rt><rb>紮的</rb><rt>tsah-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>面孔</rb><rt>min-kʽong</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>手巾</rb><rt>siu-kying</rt><rb>包的</rb><rt>pao-tih</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>解開仔</rb><rt>Ka-kʽæ-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>45</sup> <ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>馬利亞</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>46</sup> <ruby><rb>內中</rb><rt>Nen-cong</rt><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利賽</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>47</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利賽</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>聚攏</rb><rt>jü-long</rt><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>神蹟</rb><rt>jing-tsih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>弄法</rb><rt>long-fah</rt></ruby>？<sup>48</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>候</rb><rt>ʽeo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>滅掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>擄掠</rb><rt>lo-liah</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。」<sup>49</sup> <ruby><rb>內中</rb><rt>Nen-cong</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>該亞法</rb><rt>Kæ-üô-fah</rt></ruby></u>，「<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>。<sup>50</sup> <ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>忖到</rb><rt>tsʽeng-tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>合國</rb><rt>ʽeh-koh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>滅掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>阿拉個</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>益處</rb><rt>ih-cʽü</rt></ruby>。』<sup>51</sup> <ruby><rb>箇句</rb><rt>Keh-kyü</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>不過</rb><rt>peh-ko</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>sin-cü</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>。<sup>52</sup> <ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>單只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>收拾攏</rb><rt>siu-jih-long</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>散開間</rb><rt>sæn-kʽæ-kæn</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>兒囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>一個生</rb><rt>ih-ko-sang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>53</sup> <ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>Dzong</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>以後</rb><rt>yi-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>商量</rb><rt>siang-liang</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>54</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>明當當</rb><rt>ming-tông-tông</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>相近個</rb><rt>siang-gying-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>城裏</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt><rb>以法蓮</rb><rt>Yi-fah-lin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>耽擱</rb><rt>tæn-kôh</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>。

<sup>55</sup> <u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>逾越節</rb><rt>Yü-yüih-tsih</rt><rb>相近了</rb><rt>siang-gying-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>節肯個</rb><rt>tsih-kʽeng-go</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>鄉下</rb><rt>hyiang-ʽô</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>弄</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>潔淨</rb><rt>kyih-zing</rt></ruby>。<sup>56</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>尋</rb><rt>zing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>聖殿裏</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>守</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>節肯</rb><rt>tsih-kʽeng</rt><rb>𣍐</rb><rt>ve</rt></ruby>？」<sup>57</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利賽</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>號令</rb><rt>ʽao-ling</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>下落</rb><rt>ʽô-lôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。

---

### Iʽe. XII.

<ruby><rb>逾越節個</rb><rt>YÜ-YÜIH-TSIH-GO</rt><rb>六</rb><rt>loh</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯大尼</rb><rt>Pah-da-nyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>死個</rb><rt>si-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉撒路</rb><rt>Lah-sah-lu</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>活轉來</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt><rb>主顧個</rb><rt>cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>烏蕩</rb><rt>u-dông</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>辦酒</rb><rt>bæn-tsiu</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>馬大</rb><rt>Mô-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>當值</rb><rt>tông-dzih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>同席個</rb><rt>dong-zih-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉撒路</rb><rt>Lah-sah-lu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>在內</rb><rt>dzæ-nen</rt></ruby>。

<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>馬利亞</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>一斤</rb><rt>ih-kying</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>貴個</rb><rt>kyü-go</rt><rb>香油</rb><rt>hyiang-yiu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>搽</rb><rt>djô</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶穌</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>腳</rb><rt>kyiah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>頭髮</rb><rt>deo-fah</rt><rb>揩</rb><rt>kʽa</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>腳</rb><rt>kyiah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>滿間</rb><rt>mun-kæn</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>噴香了</rb><rt>pʽeng-hyiang-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> 「<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>香油</rb><rt>hyiang-yiu</rt><rb>何弗</rb><rt>ʽo-feh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>賣</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>三十兩</rb><rt>sæn-jih-liang</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>窮人</rb><rt>gyüong-nying</rt></ruby>？」<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇句</rb><rt>keh-kyü</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>並非</rb><rt>bing-fi</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>顧着</rb><rt>kwu-djôh</rt><rb>窮人</rb><rt>gyüong-nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>賊人</rb><rt>zeh-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>錢袋</rb><rt>dzin-dæ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>馱的個</rb><rt>do-tih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>囥的個</rb><rt>kʽông-tih-go</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>*nying-ts*</rt><rb>不常</rb><rt>*peh-djông*</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>偷個</rb><rt>tʽeo-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>且</rb><rt>Tsʽia</rt><rb>隨</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>囥</rb><rt>kʽông</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>香油</rb><rt>hyiang-yiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>安葬</rb><rt>en-tsông</rt><rb>日子個</rb><rt>nyih-ts-go</rt><rb>用場</rb><rt>yüong-dziang</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一大潮</rb><rt>ih-do-dziao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>單只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>活轉來</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉撒路</rb><rt>Lah-sah-lu</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>打算</rb><rt>tang-sön</rt><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉撒路</rb><rt>Lah-sah-lu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>Di-nyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>守</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>節肯</rb><rt>tsih-kʽeng</rt><rb>箇大潮</rb><rt>keh-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來了</rb><rt>læ-de</rt></ruby>，<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>棗樹個</rb><rt>tsao-jü-go</rt><rb>椏枝</rb><rt>ô-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走出來</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>迎接</rb><rt>nying-tsih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>響響</rb><rt>hyiang-hyiang</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>和散𠺶</rb><rt>ʽO-sæn-nah</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>Wông-ti</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>應該</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>祝讚個</rb><rt>coh-tsæn-go</rt></ruby>！」<sup>14</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>尋着</rb><rt>zing-djôh</rt><rb>一匹</rb><rt>ih-pʽih</rt><rb>小驢</rb><rt>siao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>騎上去</rb><rt>gyi-zông-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>書裏</rb><rt>*Shü-li*</rt><rb>寫的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<sup>15</sup> 「<ruby><rb><u>郇</u>個</rb><rt>Shing-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>Wông-ti</rt><rb>騎勒</rb><rt>gyi-leh</rt><rb>一匹</rb><rt>ih-pʽih</rt><rb>小驢</rb><rt>siao-li</rt><rb>來了</rb><rt>læ-de</rt></ruby>。」 <sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>起初</rb><rt>kyʽi-tsʽu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>之後</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>纔始</rb><rt>dzæ-s</rt><rb>記得</rb><rt>kyi-teh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>寫的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt><rb>指點</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>做過</rb><rt>tso-ko</rt></ruby>。

<sup>17</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉撒路</rb><rt>Lah-sah-lu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>墳墓</rb><rt>veng-mo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>活轉來</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來東個</rb><rt>læ-tong-go</rt><rb>箇班</rb><rt>keh-pæn</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>箇潮</rb><rt>keh-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>迎接</rb><rt>nying-tsih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>神蹟</rb><rt>jing-tsih</rt><rb>做過</rb><rt>tso-ko</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <u><ruby><rb>法利賽</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>且看</rb><rt>Tsʽia-kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>白力力</rb><rt>bah-lih-lih</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>*læ-tih*</rt><rb>打算</rb><rt>*tang-sön*</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>咸天下</rb><rt>ʽen-tʽin-ʽô</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>跟着</rb><rt>keng-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>當</rb><rt>TÔNG</rt><rb>節肯</rb><rt>tsih-kʽeng</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>上來</rb><rt>zông-læ</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>禮拜</rb><rt>li-pa</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希臘</rb><rt>Hyi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>加利利</u>個</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯賽大</rb><rt>Pah-sæ-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>腓力</rb><rt>Fi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>烏蕩</rb><rt>u-dông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>先生</rb><rt>Sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>見見</rb><rt>kyin-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>。」<sup>22</sup> <u><ruby><rb>腓力</rb><rt>Fi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>安得烈</rb><rt>En-teh-lih</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>安得烈</rb><rt>En-teh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>腓力</rb><rt>Fi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>23</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真真</rb><rt>tsing-tsing</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一粒</rb><rt>Ih-lih</rt><rb>麥</rb><rt>mah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>跌落</rb><rt>tih-lôh</rt><rb>地垟裏</rb><rt>di-yiang-li</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>仍舊</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>一粒</rb><rt>ih-lih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>爆出</rb><rt>pao-cʽih</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>粒數</rb><rt>lih-su</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>愛惜</rb><rt>Æ-sih</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>反轉</rb><rt>*fæn-cün*</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>喪掉</rb><rt>sông-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>看輕</rb><rt>kʽen-kyʽing</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>反轉</rb><rt>*fæn-cün*</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>保全</rb><rt>pao-djün</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>永遠</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇句</rb><rt>keh-kyü</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>指點</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>Gyi-zi</rt><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>死法</rb><rt>si-fah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>箇潮</rb><rt>Keh-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>書裏</rb><rt>*shü-li*</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>永遠</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>提上去</rb><rt>di-zông-kyʽi</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>？」<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>趁</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>亮光</rb><rt>liang-kwông</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>亮光</rb><rt>Liang-kwông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>光明個</rb><rt>kwông-ming-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>講好</rb><rt>kông-hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>37</sup> <sup>41</sup> <sup>44</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>響響</rb><rt>hyiang-hyiang</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>*siang-sing*</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<sup>45</sup> ### Iʽe. XIII.

<ruby><rb>逾越</rb><rt>YÜ-YÜIH</rt><rb>節肯個</rb><rt>tsih-kʽeng-go</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>去個</rb><rt>kyʽi-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其自個</rb><rt>Gyi-zi-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>來東個</rb><rt>læ-tong-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一向</rb><rt>*ih-hyiang*</rt><rb>愛惜</rb><rt>æ-sih</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>底</rb><rt>ti</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>愛惜</rb><rt>æ-sih</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>到了</rb><rt>Tao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西門</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u><u>·</u><ruby><rb><u>彼得</u>個</rb><rt>Pe-teh-go</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>滰</rb><rt>gyiang</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>腳</rb><rt>kyiah</rt></ruby>？』<sup>7</sup> <u><ruby><rb>西門</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u><u>·</u><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>單只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>腳</rb><rt>kyiah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>頭</rb><rt>deo</rt></ruby>。』<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>肉</rb><rt>Nyüoh</rt><rb>滰過了</rb><rt>gyiang-ko-liao</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>只消</rb><rt>tsih-siao</rt><rb>滰</rb><rt>gyiang</rt><rb>腳</rb><rt>kyiah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>渾身</rb><rt>weng-sing</rt><rb>乾淨</rb><rt>ken-zing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>乾淨</rb><rt>ken-zing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>不過</rb><rt>peh-ko</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt></ruby>。』<sup>11</sup> <sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>榜樣</rb><rt>pông-yiang</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>做過</rb><rt>tso-ko</rt><rb>一樣個</rb><rt>ih-yiang-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真真</rb><rt>tsing-tsing</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>Nu-boh</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>大如</rb><rt>do-jü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>差來個</rb><rt>tsʽa-læ-go</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>大如</rb><rt>do-jü</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>話個</rb><rt>wô-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>指點</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>挑選個</rb><rt>tʽiao-shün-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>曉得個</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>不過</rb><rt>peh-ko</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>聖書</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>效驗</rb><rt>yiao-nyiæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>*z-ka*</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>Teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>飯</rb><rt>væn</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>挑起</rb><rt>tʽiao-kyʽi</rt><rb>腳後跟</rb><rt>kyiah-ʽeo-keng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>踢</rb><rt>tʽih</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。』 <sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>做成</rb><rt>tso-dzing</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>做成</rb><rt>tso-dzing</rt><rb>之後</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真真</rb><rt>tsing-tsing</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>差去個</rb><rt>tsʽa-kyʽi-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。』

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, dzong-tsʽ-ʽeo. 或話，從此以後。（原註）</sup>

<sup>21</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>講好</rb><rt>kông-hao</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>難過</rb><rt>næn-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真真</rb><rt>tsing-tsing</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>賣掉</rb><rt>ma-diao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。』<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>門徒</rb><rt>Meng-du</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>忖弗出</rb><rt>tsʽeng-feh-cʽih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>戤着</rb><rt>gæ-djôh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>胸脘頭</rb><rt>hyüong-kwun-deo</rt><rb>喫飯</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-væn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>愛惜個</rb><rt>æ-sih-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <u><ruby><rb>西門</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u><u>·</u><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>嘴巴</rb><rt>cü-pô</rt><rb>噘噘</rb><rt>kyüih-kyüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>戤到</rb><rt>gæ-tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶穌</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>胸脘頭</rb><rt>hyüong-kwun-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>？』<sup>26</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>搵</rb><rt>w̆eng</rt><rb>餅</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>遞</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>搵</rb><rt>w̆eng</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>餅</rb><rt>ping</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>遞</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加略</rb><rt>Kyüô-liah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>西門</u>個</rb><rt>Si-meng-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>*ng-ts*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>\[1\] W̆eng ping; *yiu-teh* *fæn* w̆eng ih-yiang tong-si. 搵餅，有得翻搵一樣東西。（原註）</sup>

<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>餅</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>*tsih-ziu*</rt><rb>之後</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>撒但</rb><rt>Sah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>入進</rb><rt>jih-tsing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做勒</rb><rt>tso-leh</rt><rb>快點</rb><rt>kwʽa-tin</rt></ruby>。』<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>同席個</rb><rt>Dong-zih-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>嘸人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇句</rb><rt>keh-kyü</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>錢袋</rb><rt>dzin-dæ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>馱的個</rb><rt>do-tih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>道是</rb><rt>dao-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>買</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>過節</rb><rt>ko-tsih</rt><rb>用個</rb><rt>yüong-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>馱點</rb><rt>do-tin</rt><rb>銅鈿</rb><rt>dong-din</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>窮人</rb><rt>gyüong-nying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>30</sup> <u><ruby><rb>猶大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>接受了</rb><rt>tsih-ziu-leh</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>餅</rb><rt>ping</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走出了</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>*keh-go*</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>*z-ʽeo*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>夜到</rb><rt>yia-tao</rt></ruby>。

<sup>31</sup> <u><ruby><rb>猶大</rb><rt>*YIU-DA*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走出仔</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-ts</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>在於</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>在於</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>在於</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>*Ngô*</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>一晌</rb><rt>ih-zông</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>尋</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>*zin-deo*</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>去個</rb><rt>kyʽi-go</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>*keh-tsao*</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>新</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>號令</rb><rt>ʽao-ling</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>相愛</rb><rt>siang-æ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>愛惜</rb><rt>æ-sih</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>相愛</rb><rt>siang-æ</rt></ruby>。<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>相愛</rb><rt>siang-æ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>。』

<sup>36</sup> <u><ruby><rb>西門</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u><u>·</u><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>？』<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>去個</rb><rt>kyʽi-go</rt><rb>烏蕩</rb><rt>u-dông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>現在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>跟</rb><rt>keng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>跟</rb><rt>keng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。』<sup>37</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>跟</rb><rt>keng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>棄掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt></ruby>。』<sup>38</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>棄掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真真</rb><rt>tsing-tsing</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>雞</rb><rt>Kyi</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>啼個</rb><rt>di-go</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>三遭</rb><rt>sæn-tsao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>招認</rb><rt>tsiao-jing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。』」
