### Iʽe. XIV.

「<ruby><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>Ng-lah-go</rt><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>難過</rb><rt>næn-ko</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>阿爹個</rb><rt>Ah-tia-go</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>房子</rb><rt>vông-ts</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>總</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>先</rb><rt>*sin*</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道了</rb><rt>dao-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>備辦</rb><rt>be-bæn</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>備辦</rb><rt>be-bæn</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>接</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>來間個</rb><rt>læ-kæn-go</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>去個</rb><rt>kyʽi-go</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>曉得個</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>曉得個</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>5</sup> <u><ruby><rb>多馬</rb><rt>To-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>去個</rb><rt>kyʽi-go</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>認得</rb><rt>nying-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>自然</rb><rt>z-jün</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>認得</rb><rt>nying-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>從今以後</rb><rt>dzong-kying-yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>認得</rb><rt>nying-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>過了</rb><rt>ko-de</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>8</sup> <u><ruby><rb>腓力</rb><rt>Fi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>夠了</rb><rt>keo-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>9</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>腓力</rb><rt>Fi-lih</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>介星</rb><rt>ka-sing</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>認得</rb><rt>nying-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>Kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>Peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby>。』<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>難道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>在於</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>在於</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>我自</rb><rt>Ngô-zi</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。

<sup>11</sup> 「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>在於</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>在於</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>*keh-go*</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>*shih-wô*</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真真</rb><rt>tsing-tsing</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>相信</rb><rt>Siang-sing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>大如</rb><rt>do-jü</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>隨便</rb><rt>ze-bin</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>啥西</rb><rt>soh-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做成</rb><rt>tso-dzing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>在於</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>啥西</rb><rt>soh-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我自</rb><rt>Ngô-zi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做成</rb><rt>tso-dzing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> 「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>愛敬</rb><rt>æ-kying</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>保守</rb><rt>pao-siu</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>號令</rb><rt>ʽao-ling</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>保家</rb><rt>Pao-kô</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>永遠</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>庉東</rb><rt>deng-tong</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>*ziu-z*</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>道理個</rb><rt>dao-li-go</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>接受個</rb><rt>tsih-ziu-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>弗見</rb><rt>feh-kyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>認得</rb><rt>nying-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>認得</rb><rt>nying-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>庉東</rb><rt>deng-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>*ziang*</rt><rb>嘸爹嘸孃</rb><rt>m-tia-m-nyiang</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>Tsæ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>一晌</rb><rt>ih-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>活東</rb><rt>weh-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>活東</rb><rt>weh-tong</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>在於</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>在於</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>在於</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>號令</rb><rt>ʽao-ling</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>保守</rb><rt>pao-siu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>愛敬</rb><rt>æ-kying</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>愛敬</rb><rt>æ-kying</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>愛惜</rb><rt>æ-sih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>愛惜</rb><rt>æ-sih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>顯出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>我自</rb><rt>Ngô-zi</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>22</sup> <u><ruby><rb>猶大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>加略</u>個</rb><rt>Kyüô-liah-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>顯出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？」<sup>23</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>We-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>愛敬</rb><rt>æ-kying</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>保守</rb><rt>pao-siu</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>愛惜</rb><rt>æ-sih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>庉東</rb><rt>deng-tong</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>Feh</rt><rb>愛敬</rb><rt>æ-kying</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>保守</rb><rt>pao-siu</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>我自個</rb><rt>Ngô-zi-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>阿爹個</rb><rt>Ah-tia-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>25</sup> 「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>保家</rb><rt>Pao-kô</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>在於</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>差來個</rb><rt>tsʽa-læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>樣樣</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>記得到</rb><rt>kyi-teh-tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>27</sup> 「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>安耽</rb><rt>en-tæn</rt><rb>遺落</rb><rt>yi-lôh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>Zi-go</rt><rb>安耽</rb><rt>en-tæn</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>Ng-lah-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>難過</rb><rt>næn-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>愛敬</rb><rt>æ-kying</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，』<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>總</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>大如</rb><rt>do-jü</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>做成</rb><rt>tso-dzing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>做成</rb><rt>tso-dzing</rt><rb>之後</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>30</sup> 「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>以後</rb><rt>yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>嘸多少</rb><rt>m-to-siao</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>世間上個</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông-go</rt><rb>主宰</rb><rt>cü-tsæ</rt><rb>來了</rb><rt>læ-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>嘸關涉</rb><rt>m-kwæn-dzih</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>不過</rb><rt>peh-ko</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>*z-ken*</rt><rb>卻勒</rb><rt>*kyʽiah-leh*</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>*z-ka*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>愛敬</rb><rt>æ-kying</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爬起</rb><rt>Bô-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。」

### Iʽe. XV.

「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>NGÔ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>真個</rb><rt>tsing-go</rt><rb>葡萄</rb><rt>bu-dao</rt><rb>樹</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>種樹個</rb><rt>cong-jü-go</rt><rb>師父</rb><rt>S-vu</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>結</rb><rt>kyih</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>椏枝</rb><rt>ô-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>斫掉</rb><rt>tsôh-diao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>結</rb><rt>kyih</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>椏枝</rb><rt>*ô-ts*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>周捉</rb><rt>tsiu-coh</rt><rb>乾淨</rb><rt>ken-zing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>結</rb><rt>kyih</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>乾淨了</rb><rt>ken-zing-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>Iao</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>Tsing-ziang</rt><rb>一吊</rb><rt>ih-tiao</rt><rb>椏枝</rb><rt>ô-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>葡萄</rb><rt>bu-dao</rt><rb>樹個</rb><rt>jü-go</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>結</rb><rt>kyih</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>。

<sup>5</sup> 「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>葡萄</rb><rt>bu-dao</rt><rb>樹</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是個</rb><rt>*z-go*</rt><rb>椏枝</rb><rt>ô-ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>結</rb><rt>kyih</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>椏枝</rb><rt>ô-ts</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>揞勒</rb><rt>kʽang-leh</rt><rb>外頭</rb><rt>nga-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>燥掉</rb><rt>sao-diao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>收拾攏</rb><rt>siu-jih-long</rt><rb>丟勒</rb><rt>tiu-leh</rt><rb>火裏</rb><rt>ho-li</rt><rb>𤈦掉</rb><rt>me-diao</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>隨便</rb><rt>ze-bin</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>啥西</rb><rt>soh-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>總</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>做成</rb><rt>tso-dzing</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>結</rb><rt>kyih</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, ziah-z teng Ngô kah-kʽæ. 或話，若是等我隔開。（原註）</sup>

<sup>9</sup> 「<ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>Tsing-ziang</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>愛惜</rb><rt>æ-sih</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>愛惜</rb><rt>æ-sih</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>常常</rb><rt>djông-djông</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>愛惜</rb><rt>æ-sih</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>保守</rb><rt>pao-siu</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>號令</rb><rt>ʽao-ling</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>愛惜</rb><rt>æ-sih</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>保守</rb><rt>pao-siu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>阿爹個</rb><rt>Ah-tia-go</rt><rb>號令</rb><rt>ʽao-ling</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>愛惜</rb><rt>æ-sih</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>滿足</rb><rt>mun-tsoh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> 「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>相愛</rb><rt>siang-æ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>愛惜</rb><rt>æ-sih</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>號令</rb><rt>ʽao-ling</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>一個</rb><rt>Ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>朋友</rb><rt>beng-yiu</rt><rb>棄掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>大如</rb><rt>do-jü</rt><rb>箇株介</rb><rt>keh-cü-ka</rt><rb>愛惜個</rb><rt>æ-sih-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>以後</rb><rt>yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>朋友</rb><rt>beng-yiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>聽聞個</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-go</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>Feh-z</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>揀選</rb><rt>kæn-shün</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>揀選</rb><rt>kæn-shün</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>設立</rb><rt>shih-lih</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>結</rb><rt>kyih</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>長通</rb><rt>dziang-tʽong</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>隨便</rb><rt>ze-bin</rt><rb>啥西</rb><rt>soh-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>相愛</rb><rt>siang-æ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>18</sup> 「<ruby><rb>世間上</rb><rt>SHÜ-KÆN-ZÔNG</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>惡愫</rb><rt>u-su</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>惡愫</rb><rt>u-su</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>話過</rb><rt>wô-ko</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>Nu-boh</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>大如</rb><rt>do-jü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt></ruby>，』<ruby><rb>箇句</rb><rt>keh-kyü</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>記得</rb><rt>kyi-teh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>逼難</rb><rt>pih-næn</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>逼難</rb><rt>pih-næn</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>保守</rb><rt>pao-siu</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>保守</rb><rt>pao-siu</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>*shih-wô*</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>一切個</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>害</rb><rt>ʽæ</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。

<sup>22</sup> 「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>講過</rb><rt>kông-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>嘸告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>罪名</rb><rt>ze-ming</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>罪名</rb><rt>ze-ming</rt><rb>嘸告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>推託</rb><rt>tʽe-tʽôh</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>惡愫</rb><rt>U-su</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>惡愫</rb><rt>u-su</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>Tsih-z</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>有個</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>書裏</rb><rt>shü-li</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>寫的個</rb><rt>sia-tih-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>效驗</rb><rt>yiao-nyiæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>*ziu-z*</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>無緣無故</rb><rt>vu-yün-vu-kwu</rt><rb>惡愫</rb><rt>u-su</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。』

<sup>26</sup> 「<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>差來</rb><rt>tsʽa-læ</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>保家</rb><rt>Pao-kô</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>出來</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>道理個</rb><rt>dao-li-go</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>起頭</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。」

### Iʽe. XVI.

「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>𢲔跌</rb><rt>pæn-tih</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>趕</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>聚會堂</rb><rt>jü-we-dông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>非但</rb><rt>fi-dæn</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>來其</rb><rt>læ-gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>殺</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>介個</rb><rt>ka-go</rt><rb>相貌</rb><rt>siang-mao</rt><rb>害</rb><rt>ʽæ</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>記得</rb><rt>kyi-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>講過了</rb><rt>kông-ko-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>以前</rb><rt>yi-zin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。

<sup>5</sup> 「<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>主顧個</rb><rt>Cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>嘸人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>？<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>滿心</rb><rt>mun-sing</rt><rb>憂悶</rb><rt>iu-meng</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>實惠</rb><rt>jih-we</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>*ng-lah*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>益處</rb><rt>ih-cʽü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>*ng-lah*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>保家</rb><rt>Pao-kô</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>公義</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>審判個</rb><rt>sing-pʽun-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>責罰</rb><rt>tsah-vah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>審判</rb><rt>sing-pʽun</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>世間上個</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông-go</rt><rb>主宰</rb><rt>cü-tsæ</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>判斷了</rb><rt>pʽun-tön-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> 「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>NGÔ</rt><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>現在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>喫弗落個</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-feh-lôh-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>道理個</rb><rt>dao-li-go</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>樣樣</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>進去</rb><rt>tsing-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>聽聞個</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>未來個</rb><rt>vi-læ-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有個</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有個</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>馱來</rb><rt>do-læ</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>庉</rb><rt>Deng</rt><rb>一晌</rb><rt>ih-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>一晌</rb><rt>ih-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>庉</rb><rt>Deng</rt><rb>一晌</rb><rt>ih-zông</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>一晌</rb><rt>ih-zông</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。』——<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>？」<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>庉</rb><rt>Deng</rt><rb>一晌</rb><rt>ih-zông</rt></ruby>，』<ruby><rb>箇句</rb><rt>keh-kyü</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>到底</rb><rt>tao-ti</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>啥西</rb><rt>soh-si</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>19</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>庉</rb><rt>Deng</rt><rb>一晌</rb><rt>ih-zông</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>一晌</rb><rt>ih-zông</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，』<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>議論</rb><rt>nyi-leng</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真真</rb><rt>tsing-tsing</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>哭</rb><rt>kʽoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>眼淚</rb><rt>ngæn-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>憂悶</rb><rt>iu-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>憂悶</rb><rt>iu-meng</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>變</rb><rt>pin</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>Nyü-nying</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>生姆</rb><rt>sang-m</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>憂悶</rb><rt>iu-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>奶花</rb><rt>na-hwun</rt><rb>生出了</rb><rt>sang-cʽih-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>前</rb><rt>zin</rt><rb>忘記</rb><rt>mông-kyi</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>苦難</rb><rt>kwʽu-næn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>生出東</rb><rt>sang-cʽih-tong</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>是介個</rb><rt>Z-ka-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>憂悶</rb><rt>iu-meng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>嘸人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>奪勒去</rb><rt>deh-leh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> ### Iʽe. XVII.

<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>YIÆ-SU</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>講好</rb><rt>kông-hao</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>抬起</rb><rt>dæ-kyʽi</rt><rb>向着</rb><rt>hyiang-djôh</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

「<ruby><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>過了</rb><rt>ko-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>完全了</rb><rt>wun-djün-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>天地</rb><rt>tʽin-di</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有個</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> 「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>顯出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>本來</rb><rt>*peng-læ*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>馱來</rb><rt>do-læ</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>保守</rb><rt>pao-siu</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> 「<ruby><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>來間個</rb><rt>læ-kæn-go</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>開闢</rb><rt>kʽæ-bih</rt><rb>天地</rb><rt>tʽin-di</rt><rb>以前</rb><rt>yi-zin</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>愛惜</rb><rt>æ-sih</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>正直個</rb><rt>Tsing-dzih-go</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>顯出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>顯出來</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>愛惜</rb><rt>æ-sih</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>。」

---

### Iʽe. XVIII.

<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>YIÆ-SU</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>講好</rb><rt>kông-hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>同隊</rb><rt>dong-de</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>過</rb><rt>ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>汲淪</rb><rt>Kyih-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>溪坑</rb><rt>kyʽi-kʽang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>花園</rb><rt>hwô-yün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>走進去</rb><rt>tseo-tsing-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>Iao</rt><rb>賣</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>掉</rb><rt>diao</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>箇𡍲</rb><rt>keh-tʽah</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>不常</rb><rt>peh-djông</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>聚攏</rb><rt>jü-long</rt></ruby>。

<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>猶大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>帶勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt><rb>一隊</rb><rt>ih-de</rt><rb>兵</rb><rt>ping</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利賽</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>差人</rb><rt>tsʽa-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>燈籠</rb><rt>teng-long</rt><rb>火把</rb><rt>ho-pô</rt><rb>刀槍</rb><rt>tao-tsʽiang</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>臨</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>着</rb><rt>djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走出來</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>*læ*</rt><rb>尋</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>？」<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb><u>拿撒勒</u>個</rb><rt>Nô-sah-leh-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>。」<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>賣</rb><rt>Ma</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>掉個</rb><rt>diao-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>立間</rb><rt>lih-kæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>倒退</rb><rt>tao-tʽe</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>跌倒</rb><rt>tih-tao</rt><rb>地垟</rb><rt>di-yiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>*læ*</rt><rb>尋</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb><u>拿撒勒</u>個</rb><rt>Nô-sah-leh-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>。」<sup>8</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>話過</rb><rt>wô-ko</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>*læ*</rt><rb>尋</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>Z-ka</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>前頭</rb><rt>*zin-deo*</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>失掉</rb><rt>shih-diao</rt></ruby>」<ruby><rb>箇句</rb><rt>keh-kyü</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>效驗</rb><rt>yiao-nyiæn</rt></ruby>。

<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>西門</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u><u>·</u><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一把</rb><rt>ih-pô</rt><rb>刀</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>帶的</rb><rt>ta-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>拔出</rb><rt>bah-cʽih</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>斬</rb><rt>tsæn</rt><rb>祭司頭個</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>削落</rb><rt>siah-lôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>順隻</rb><rt>jing-tsah</rt><rb>耳朵</rb><rt>ng-to</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>奴僕個</rb><rt>nu-boh-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>馬勒古</rb><rt>Mô-leh-kwu</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>11</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>刀</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>套進</rb><rt>tʽao-tsing</rt><rb>刀殼裏</rb><rt>tao-kʽôh-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>杯子</rb><rt>pe-ts</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>難道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>KEH-TSAO</rt><rb>箇隊</rb><rt>keh-de</rt><rb>兵</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>千總</rb><rt>tsʽin-tsong</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>差人</rb><rt>tsʽa-nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>縛仔</rb><rt>bo-ts</rt></ruby>，<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞拿</rb><rt>Üô-nô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>該亞法</u>個</rb><rt>Kæ-üô-fah-go</rt><rb>丈人</rb><rt>dziang-nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <u><ruby><rb>該亞法</rb><rt>Kæ-üô-fah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>一個</rb><rt>Ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>阿拉個</rb><rt>*ah-lah-go*</rt><rb>便宜</rb><rt>bin-i</rt></ruby>」<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> <u><ruby><rb>西門</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u><u>·</u><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>別個</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>keng-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>認得個</rb><rt>nying-teh-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>祭司頭個</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo-go</rt><rb>衙門裏</rb><rt>ngô-meng-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>門口</rb><rt>meng-kʽeo</rt><rb>外頭</rb><rt>nga-deo</rt><rb>立間</rb><rt>lih-kæn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>Tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>認得個</rb><rt>nying-teh-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>走出來</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>管門個</rb><rt>kwun-meng-go</rt><rb>丫頭</rb><rt>*ô-deo*</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>一聲</rb><rt>ih-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>帶勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>進去</rb><rt>tsing-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>管門個</rb><rt>Kwun-meng-go</rt><rb>丫頭</rb><rt>ô-deo</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt></ruby>。」<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>差人</rb><rt>tsʽa-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>天家</rb><rt>tʽin-kô</rt><rb>冷</rb><rt>lang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>炭火</rb><rt>tʽæn-ho</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>立仔</rb><rt>lih-ts</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>烘</rb><rt>hong</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>立仔</rb><rt>lih-ts</rt><rb>烘烘</rb><rt>hong-hong</rt></ruby>。

<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>Tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>Gyi-zi</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>問問</rb><rt>meng-meng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>20</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>明當當</rb><rt>ming-tông-tông</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>長通</rb><rt>dziang-tʽong</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>聚會堂</rb><rt>jü-we-dông</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>聖殿裏</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>常常</rb><rt>djông-djông</rt><rb>聚攏個</rb><rt>jü-long-go</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>私下</rb><rt>s-ʽô</rt><rb>烏蕩</rb><rt>u-dông</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>講過</rb><rt>kông-ko</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>聽聞個</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>啥西</rb><rt>soh-si</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>曉得個</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>正好</rb><rt>tsing-hao</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>旁邊</rb><rt>bông-pin</rt><rb>立東個</rb><rt>lih-tong-go</rt><rb>差人</rb><rt>tsʽa-nying</rt><rb>摑</rb><rt>kwah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一光</rb><rt>ih-kwông</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>應</rb><rt>ing</rt><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<sup>23</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>講錯了</rb><rt>kông-tsʽo-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>批評</rb><rt>pʽi-bing</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>錯</rb><rt>tsʽo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>錯</rb><rt>tsʽo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, s bông tang Yiæ-su ih-kyi. 或話，使棒打<u>耶穌</u>一記。（原註）</sup>

<sup>24</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>仍舊</rb><rt>*dzing-gyiu*</rt><rb>縛了個</rb><rt>bo-liao-go</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>亞拿</rb><rt>Üô-nô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>送去</rb><rt>song-kyʽi</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>該亞法</rb><rt>Kæ-üô-fah</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>25</sup> <u><ruby><rb>西門</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u><u>·</u><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>立仔</rb><rt>lih-ts</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>烘火</rb><rt>hong-ho</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>招認</rb><rt>tsiao-jing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>祭司頭個</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>削落</rb><rt>siah-lôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>耳朵</rb><rt>ng-to</rt><rb>主顧個</rb><rt>cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>親眷</rb><rt>tsʽing-kyün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>花園</rb><rt>hwô-yün</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<sup>27</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>招認</rb><rt>tsiao-jing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>雞</rb><rt>kyi</rt><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>啼了</rb><rt>di-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>KEH-TSAO</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>該亞法</u>個</rb><rt>Kæ-üô-fah-go</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>公館裏</rb><rt>kong-kwun-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。<ruby><rb>時候</rb><rt>Z-ʽeo</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>早</rb><rt>tsao</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>黵着</rb><rt>tæn-djôh</rt><rb>鏖糟</rb><rt>ao-tsao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>公館裏</rb><rt>kong-kwun-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>逾越</rb><rt>yü-yüih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, Pe-lah-to ngô-meng: *kʽen* Mt. 27. 27. 或話，<u>彼拉多</u>衙門。看 Mt. 27. 27.（原註）</sup>

<sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走出來</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>案件</rb><rt>en-gyin</rt></ruby>？」<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>壞</rb><rt>w̆a</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>做過</rb><rt>tso-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>再𣍐</rb><rt>tsæ-ve</rt><rb>送來</rb><rt>song-læ</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。」<sup>31</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>帶勒去</rb><rt>ta-leh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>判斷</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>殺人個</rb><rt>sah-nying-go</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt></ruby>。」<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>S-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>指點</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>Gyi-zi</rt><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>死法</rb><rt>si-fah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>效驗</rb><rt>yiao-nyiæn</rt></ruby>。

<sup>33</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>公館裏</rb><rt>kong-kwun-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>Wông-ti</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<sup>34</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇句</rb><rt>Keh-kyü</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爾自</rb><rt>ng-zi</rt><rb>講出來個</rb><rt>kông-cʽih-læ-go</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>還是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>別人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>道個</rb><rt>dao-go</rt></ruby>？」<sup>35</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>*z-ka*</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>爾自個</rb><rt>Ng-zi-go</rt><rb>本國人</rb><rt>peng-koh-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>送來</rb><rt>song-læ</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>到底</rb><rt>tao-ti</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>？」<sup>36</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>*z-ka*</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>國</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>屬於</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>國</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>屬於</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>手下人</rb><rt>siu-ʽô-nying</rt><rb>總</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>出力</rb><rt>cʽih-lih</rt><rb>打仗</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>省勒</rb><rt>sæn-leh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>國</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>屬於</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt></ruby>。」<sup>37</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>話着了</rb><rt>wô-djôh-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>Wông-ti</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>生出來個</rb><rt>sang-cʽih-læ-go</rt><rb>緣故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>緣故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>道理個</rb><rt>dao-li-go</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>屬於</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>38</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>啥西</rb><rt>Soh-si</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>？」

<ruby><rb>話勒</rb><rt>Wô-leh</rt><rb>一聲</rb><rt>ih-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>走出去</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>嘸告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>。<sup>39</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>老</rb><rt>lao</rt><rb>規矩</rb><rt>kwe-kyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>逾越節</rb><rt>Yü-yüih-tsih</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>犯人</rb><rt>væn-nying</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>Wông-ti</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>衆人</rb><rt>Cong-nying</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>懷懷響</rb><rt>wæ-wæ-hyiang</rt><rb>謳起來</rb><rt>eo-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>Feh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>*fông*</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>放</rb><rt>*fông*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴拉巴</rb><rt>Pô-lah-pô</rt></ruby></u>。」<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴拉巴</rb><rt>Pô-lah-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>強盜</rb><rt>gyiang-dao</rt></ruby>。
