### Iʽe. XIX.

<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>使</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>鞭子</rb><rt>pin-ts</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>兵</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>刺</rb><rt>tsʽ</rt><rb>編出</rb><rt>pin-cʽih</rt><rb>一頂</rb><rt>ih-ting</rt><rb>平天冠</rb><rt>bing-tʽin-kwun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>戴</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>頭裏</rb><rt>deo-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>紅袍</rb><rt>ʽong-bao</rt><rb>披勒</rb><rt>pʽi-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<sup>3</sup> 「<u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>Wông-ti</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恭喜</rb><rt>kong-hyi</rt></ruby>！」<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>馱起</rb><rt>do-kyʽi</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>4</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>出來</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>嘸告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>帶勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt><rb>刺</rb><rt>tsʽ</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>平天冠</rb><rt>bing-tʽin-kwun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>披勒</rb><rt>pʽi-leh</rt><rb>紅袍</rb><rt>ʽong-bao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走出來</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>*Pe-lah-to*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>且看</rb><rt>Tsʽia-kʽen</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt></ruby>！」<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>差人</rb><rt>tsʽa-nying</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>懷懷響</rb><rt>wæ-wæ-hyiang</rt><rb>謳起來</rb><rt>eo-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>釘</rb><rt>Ting</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*Gyi*</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>十字架上</rb><rt>jih-z-kô-zông</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>釘</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*Gyi*</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>十字架上</rb><rt>jih-z-kô-zông</rt></ruby>！」<u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>釘</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>十字架上</rb><rt>jih-z-kô-zông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>嘸告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>。」<sup>7</sup> <u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一條</rb><rt>ih-diao</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>Gyi-zi</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>8</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇句</rb><rt>keh-kyü</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>越發</rb><rt>yüih-fah</rt><rb>怕起來了</rb><rt>pʽô-kyʽi-læ-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>官廳裏</rb><rt>kwun-tʽing-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>到底</rb><rt>tao-ti</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt></ruby>？」<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>應</rb><rt>ing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>難道</rb><rt>Næn-dao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>釘</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>十字架上</rb><rt>jih-z-kô-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>釋放</rb><rt>sih-fông</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<sup>11</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>上頭</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>嘸告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>辦</rb><rt>bæn</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>打算</rb><rt>tang-sön</rt><rb>釋放</rb><rt>sih-fông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>謳起來</rb><rt>eo-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>釋放</rb><rt>sih-fông</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>該撒</u>個</rb><rt>Kæ-sah-go</rt><rb>忠臣</rb><rt>cong-dzing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>自立爲王</rb><rt>zi-lih-we-wông</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>得罪</rb><rt>teh-ze</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>該撒</rb><rt>Kæ-sah</rt></ruby></u>。」<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>帶勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>出來</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>座位裏</rb><rt>zo-we-li</rt><rb>坐仔</rb><rt>zo-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>一𡍲</rb><rt>ih-tʽah</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt><rb>鋪華石</rb><rt>Pʽu-wô-zah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>希伯來</u>個</rb><rt>Hyi-pah-læ-go</rt><rb>口音</rb><rt>kʽeo-ing</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>*kyiao-leh*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>厄巴大</rb><rt>Eh-pô-da</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>14</sup> （<ruby><rb>時候</rb><rt>*z-ʽeo*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>預備</rb><rt>yü-be</rt><rb>逾越節</rb><rt>Yü-yüih-tsih</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>約來</rb><rt>iah-læ</rt><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>點鐘</rb><rt>tin-cong</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。）<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>且看</rb><rt>Tsʽia-kʽen</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>Wông-ti</rt></ruby>！」<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>懷懷響</rb><rt>wæ-wæ-hyiang</rt><rb>謳起來</rb><rt>eo-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>棄掉</rb><rt>Kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*Gyi*</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>棄掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*Gyi*</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>釘</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>十字架上</rb><rt>jih-z-kô-zông</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>Ng-lah-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>Wông-ti</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>釘</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>十字架上</rb><rt>jih-z-kô-zông</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>*z-ka*</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>除了</rb><rt>Djü-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>該撒</rb><rt>Kæ-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>之外</rb><rt>ts-nga</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt></ruby>。」<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>*Pe-lah-to*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>釘</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>十字架上</rb><rt>jih-z-kô-zông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *Yiu-teh* *fæn*, loh tin-cong. *Yiu-kyi-go* *kwu-kyün* *wô*, kyiu tin-cong. 有得翻，六點鐘。有幾個古卷話，九點鐘。（原註）</sup>

<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>GYI-LAH</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>帶勒去了</rb><rt>ta-leh-kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>揹勒</rb><rt>pe-leh</rt><rb>十字架</rb><rt>jih-z-kô</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>一𡍲</rb><rt>ih-tʽah</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt><rb>骷顱頭</rb><rt>Kwʽu-lu-deo</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>希伯來</u>個</rb><rt>Hyi-pah-læ-go</rt><rb>口音</rb><rt>kʽeo-ing</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>各各他</rb><rt>Kôh-kôh-tʽa</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>釘</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>十字架上</rb><rt>jih-z-kô-zông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>釘間</rb><rt>*ting-kæn*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一個</rb><rt>*ih-go*</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>蕩邊</rb><rt>dông-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一個</rb><rt>*ih-go*</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇邊</rb><rt>keh-pin</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>19</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>寫</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>一張</rb><rt>ih-tsiang</rt><rb>條子</rb><rt>diao-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>貼</rb><rt>tʽiah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>十字架</rb><rt>jih-z-kô</rt><rb>上頭</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>寫的個</rb><rt>sia-tih-go</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb><u>拿撒勒</u>個</rb><rt>NÔ-SAH-LEH-GO</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>YIÆ-SU</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>YIU-TʽA</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>NYING-GO</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>WÔNG-TI</rt></ruby>。」<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>讀</rb><rt>doh</rt><rb>箇張</rb><rt>keh-tsiang</rt><rb>條子</rb><rt>diao-ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>釘</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>十字架</rb><rt>jih-z-kô</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>城裏</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>相近</rb><rt>siang-gying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希伯來</rb><rt>Hyi-pah-læ</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>希臘</rb><rt>Hyi-lah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb><u>羅馬</u>個</rb><rt>Lo-mô-go</rt><rb>字眼</rb><rt>z-ngæn</rt><rb>寫個</rb><rt>sia-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>甮</rb><rt>Vong</rt><rb>寫</rb><rt>sia</rt></ruby>，『<u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>Wông-ti</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>寫</rb><rt>sia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其自</rb><rt>Gyi-zi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>Wông-ti</rt></ruby>。』」<sup>22</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>寫</rb><rt>sia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>寫的了</rb><rt>sia-tih-de</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>兵</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>既經</rb><rt>kyi-kying</rt><rb>釘</rb><rt>ting</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>十字架上</rb><rt>jih-z-kô-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>四股</rb><rt>s-kwu</rt><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>每人</rb><rt>me-nying</rt><rb>各</rb><rt>kôh</rt><rb>得</rb><rt>teh</rt><rb>一股</rb><rt>ih-kwu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>*do-leh*</rt><rb>替裏</rb><rt>tʽi-li</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>替裏</rb><rt>tʽi-li</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>縫烏</rb><rt>vong-u</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>下</rb><rt>ʽô</rt><rb>做出</rb><rt>tso-cʽih</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>一件生</rb><rt>ih-gyin-sang</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>甮</rb><rt>vong</rt><rb>扯開</rb><rt>cʽô-kʽæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>撮</rb><rt>tsʽeh</rt><rb>指末頭</rb><rt>ts-meh-den</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>箇節</rb><rt>keh-tsih</rt><rb>聖書</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>效驗</rb><rt>yiao-nyiæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>外頭</rb><rt>nga-deo</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>分開</rb><rt>feng-kʽæ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>裏頭</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>撮</rb><rt>tsʽeh</rt><rb>指末頭</rb><rt>ts-meh-den</rt></ruby>。」 <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>兵</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。

<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb><u>耶穌</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>阿孃個</rb><rt>ah-nyiang-go</rt><rb>姊妹</rb><rt>tsi-me</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>革羅巴</u><sup> </sup>個</rb><rt>Keh-lo-pô-go</rt><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>馬利亞</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>抹大拉</u>個</rb><rt>Meh-da-lah-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>馬利亞</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>十字架</rb><rt>jih-z-kô</rt><rb>旁邊</rb><rt>bông-pin</rt><rb>立間</rb><rt>lih-kæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>愛惜個</rb><rt>æ-sih-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>旁邊</rb><rt>bông-pin</rt><rb>立東</rb><rt>lih-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>Nyü-nying</rt></ruby>，」<ruby><rb>且看</rb><rt>tsʽia-kʽen</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>。」<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>且看</rb><rt>Tsʽia-kʽen</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>Dzong</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>接</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>*oh-li*</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh-tsia*, Keh-liu-pô-go. 或者，<u>革流巴</u>個。（原註）</sup>

<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>樣樣</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>完全了</rb><rt>wun-djün-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>聖書</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>效驗</rb><rt>yiao-nyiæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>口渴</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeh</rt></ruby>。」<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>*Keh-deo*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>滿滿</rb><rt>mun-mun</rt><rb>一瓶</rb><rt>ih-bing</rt><rb>酸酒</rb><rt>sön-tsiu</rt><rb>安間</rb><rt>en-kæn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>海綿花</rb><rt>hæ-min-hwô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt><rb>滲透仔</rb><rt>seng-tʽeo-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>戳</rb><rt>cʽoh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>牛膝</rb><rt>ngeo-sih</rt><rb>草裏</rb><rt>tsʽao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>遞</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>嘴巴</rb><rt>cü-pô</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶</rb><rt>Yiæ</rt><rb>穌</rb><rt>su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>受勒</rb><rt>ziu-leh</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>酸酒</rb><rt>sön-tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>事幹</rb><rt>Z-ken</rt><rb>完全了</rb><rt>wun-djün-de</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>頭</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>傴倒</rb><rt>eo-tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>Zi-go</rt><rb>活靈</rb><rt>weh-ling</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>天父</rb><rt>*Tʽin-Vu*</rt></ruby>。

<sup>31</sup> <u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>*keh*</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>*nyih*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>預備日</rb><rt>yü-be-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>省勒</rb><rt>sæn-leh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>屍首</rb><rt>s-siu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt><rb>掛</rb><rt>kwô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>十字架上</rb><rt>jih-z-kô-zông</rt></ruby>，（<ruby><rb>再話</rb><rt>tsæ-wô</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt></ruby>，）<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>應許</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt><rb>敲斷</rb><rt>kʽao-dön</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>腳骨</rb><rt>kyiah-kweh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>馱過</rb><rt>do-ko</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>屍首</rb><rt>*s-siu*</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>兵</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>敲斷</rb><rt>kʽao-dön</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>兩</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>同隊</rb><rt>dong-de</rt><rb>釘間</rb><rt>ting-kæn</rt><rb>主顧個</rb><rt>cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>腳骨</rb><rt>kyiah-kweh</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>到了</rb><rt>Tao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>敲斷</rb><rt>kʽao-dön</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>腳骨</rb><rt>kyiah-kweh</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>兵</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>一支</rb><rt>ih-ts</rt><rb>槍</rb><rt>tsʽiang</rt><rb>戳進</rb><rt>cʽoh-tsing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>腰脅裏</rb><rt>iao-hyih-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>流出來</rb><rt>liu-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>Tso</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>親眼</rb><rt>*tsʽing-ngæn*</rt><rb>看見過個</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-ko-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>實惠個</rb><rt>jih-we-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>真話</rb><rt>tsing-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>做法</rb><rt>tso-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>箇節</rb><rt>keh-tsih</rt><rb>聖書</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>效驗</rb><rt>yiao-nyiæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>骨頭</rb><rt>kweh-deo</rt><rb>一根</rb><rt>ih-keng</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>敲斷</rb><rt>kʽao-dön</rt></ruby>。」 <sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>聖書</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>一節</rb><rt>ih-tsih</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>戳傷</rb><rt>cʽoh-sông</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。」 

<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>KEH-SING</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>亞利馬太</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-li-mô-tʽa-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u>，（<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶穌</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>不過</rb><rt>peh-ko</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>私下</rb><rt>s-ʽô</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>，）<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>*keh-go*</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>*nying*</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶穌</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>屍首</rb><rt>s-siu</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>應許了</rb><rt>ing-hyü-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶穌</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>屍首</rb><rt>s-siu</rt><rb>馱勒去</rb><rt>do-leh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>39</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>尼哥底母</rb><rt>Nyi-ko-ti-meo</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>夜到</rb><rt>yia-tao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>見</rb><rt>kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>帶勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt><rb>靠百斤</rb><rt>kʽao-pah-kying</rt><rb>沒藥</rb><rt>meh-yiah</rt><rb>和勒</rb><rt>ʽo-leh</rt><rb>蘆薈</rb><rt>lu-we</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>葬個</rb><rt>tsông-go</rt><rb>規矩</rb><rt>kwe-kyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶穌</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>屍首</rb><rt>s-siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>麻布</rb><rt>mô-pu</rt><rb>安勒</rb><rt>en-leh</rt><rb>香料</rb><rt>hyiang-liao</rt><rb>裹帶攏</rb><rt>ko-tæn-long</rt></ruby>。

<sup>41</sup> <ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>釘</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>十字架</rb><rt>jih-z-kô</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>花園</rb><rt>hwô-yün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>花園</rb><rt>hwô-yün</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一穴</rb><rt>ih-yüih</rt><rb>壽墳</rb><rt>ziu-veng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>葬過</rb><rt>tsông-ko</rt></ruby>。<sup>42</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>*Keh*</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>*nyih*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>預備日</rb><rt>yü-be-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>再話</rb><rt>tsæ-wô</rt><rb>箇穴</rb><rt>keh-yüih</rt><rb>墳</rb><rt>veng</rt><rb>近便</rb><rt>gying-bin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>葬</rb><rt>tsông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>。

### Iʽe. XX.

<ruby><rb>七</rb><rt>TSʽIH</rt><rb>日個</rb><rt>NYIH-GO</rt><rb>頭日</rb><rt>deo-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>黑早</rb><rt>heh-tsao</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>抹大拉</u>個</rb><rt>Meh-da-lah-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>馬利亞</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>墳頭</rb><rt>veng-deo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>墳</rb><rt>veng</rt><rb>門口</rb><rt>meng-kʽeo</rt><rb>箇塊</rb><rt>keh-kwʽe</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>移過了</rb><rt>yi-ko-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>奔去</rb><rt>peng-kyʽi</rt><rb>尋着</rb><rt>zing-djôh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西門</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u><u>·</u><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>別個</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>愛惜個</rb><rt>æ-sih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>主個</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>屍首</rb><rt>*s-siu*</rt><rb>墳裏</rb><rt>veng-li</rt><rb>馱出</rb><rt>do-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>安</rb><rt>en</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>別個</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>墳頭</rb><rt>veng-deo</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>兩個</rb><rt>Liang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>平齊</rb><rt>bing-zi</rt><rb>奔勒去</rb><rt>peng-leh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>別個</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>奔勒</rb><rt>peng-leh</rt><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>墳頭</rb><rt>veng-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>傴倒</rb><rt>eo-tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>麻布</rb><rt>mô-pu</rt><rb>安間</rb><rt>en-kæn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>走進去</rb><rt>tseo-tsing-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>西門</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u><u>·</u><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>隨後</rb><rt>ze-ʽeo</rt><rb>趕到</rb><rt>ken-tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>墳</rb><rt>veng</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>麻布</rb><rt>mô-pu</rt><rb>安間</rb><rt>en-kæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>箇塊</rb><rt>keh-kwʽe</rt><rb>包</rb><rt>pao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>頭</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>手巾</rb><rt>siu-kying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>麻布</rb><rt>mô-pu</rt><rb>做𡍲</rb><rt>tso-tʽah</rt><rb>安間</rb><rt>en-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>另外</rb><rt>ling-nga</rt><rb>一𡍲</rb><rt>ih-tʽah</rt><rb>摺攏間</rb><rt>tsih-long-kæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>別個</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>墳頭</rb><rt>veng-deo</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>走進去</rb><rt>tseo-tsing-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>相信了</rb><rt>siang-sing-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt><rb>聖書</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>活轉來</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>兩</rb><rt>*liang*</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>11</sup> <u><ruby><rb>馬利亞</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>仍舊</rb><rt>*dzing-gyiu*</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>墳</rb><rt>veng</rt><rb>門口</rb><rt>meng-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>哭</rb><rt>kʽoh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>哭個</rb><rt>Kʽoh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>朝勒</rb><rt>dziao-leh</rt><rb>墳</rb><rt>veng</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>傴倒</rb><rt>eo-tao</rt><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>兩</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>穿</rb><rt>cʽün</rt><rb>白白個</rb><rt>bah-bah-go</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>安</rb><rt>en</rt><rb>屍首</rb><rt>s-siu</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>坐間</rb><rt>zo-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>頭沿</rb><rt>deo-yin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>腳沿</rb><rt>kyiah-yin</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>天使</rb><rt>Tʽin-s</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>Nyü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>哭</rb><rt>kʽoh</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>主個</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>屍首</rb><rt>s-siu</rt><rb>馱勒去</rb><rt>do-leh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗照</rb><rt>feh-tsiao</rt><rb>安</rb><rt>en</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt></ruby>。」<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>講好</rb><rt>kông-hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>扭轉</rb><rt>nyin-cün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>立東</rb><rt>lih-tong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>15</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>Nyü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>哭</rb><rt>kʽoh</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>尋</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>？」<u><ruby><rb>馬利亞</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>道是</rb><rt>dao-z</rt><rb>管園個</rb><rt>kwun-yün-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>先生</rb><rt>Sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>屍首</rb><rt>*s-siu*</rt><rb>馱過了</rb><rt>do-ko-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>安間</rb><rt>en-kæn</rt><rb>話話</rb><rt>wô-wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby>。」<sup>16</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>馬利亞</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>！」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>扭轉</rb><rt>nyin-cün</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>拉波尼</rb><rt>Lah-po-nyi</rt></ruby>！」（<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>「<ruby><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt></ruby>」<ruby><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt></ruby>。）<sup>17</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>M-nao</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>按</rb><rt>en</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>升上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>升上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。』」

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb><u>抹大拉</u>個</rb><rt>Meh-da-lah-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>馬利亞</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>*Cü*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>講過了</rb><rt>kông-ko-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>當日</rb><rt>TÔNG-NYIH</rt><rb>夜快</rb><rt>yia-kwʽa</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>七日個</rb><rt>tsʽih-nyih-go</rt><rb>頭日</rb><rt>deo-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>聚攏間個</rb><rt>jü-long-kæn-go</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>門</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>關了個</rb><rt>kwæn-liao-go</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>立勒</rb><rt>lih-leh</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>Tsʽing</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>平安</rb><rt>bing-en</rt></ruby>！」<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>箇句</rb><rt>Keh-kyü</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>講好</rb><rt>kông-hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>腰脅</rb><rt>iao-hyih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>門徒</rb><rt>Meng-du</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>Tsʽing</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>平安</rb><rt>bing-en</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>*næn-kæn*</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>說話</rb><rt>Shih-wô</rt><rb>講好</rb><rt>kông-hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>颬</rb><rt>hô</rt><rb>一口</rb><rt>ih-kʽeo</rt><rb>氣</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>赦掉</rb><rt>sô-diao</rt><rb>誰個</rb><rt>jü-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>赦掉了</rb><rt>sô-diao-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>留住</rb><rt>liu-djü</rt><rb>誰個</rb><rt>jü-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>*ze*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>*gyi-lah*</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>*ze*</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>留住了</rb><rt>liu-djü-de</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>十二</rb><rt>Jih-nyi</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>多馬</rb><rt>To-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>低土馬</rb><rt>Ti-tʽu-mô</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>餘多</rb><rt>Yü-to</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>過了</rb><rt>ko-de</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>多馬</rb><rt>To-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>手裏個</rb><rt>siu-li-go</rt><rb>釘子</rb><rt>ting-cü</rt><rb>烏</rb><rt>u</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>指頭</rb><rt>ts-deo</rt><rb>摸着</rb><rt>moh-djôh</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>釘子</rb><rt>ting-cü</rt><rb>烏</rb><rt>u</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>摸着</rb><rt>moh-djôh</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>腰脅裏</rb><rt>iao-hyih-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>總</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>過了</rb><rt>Ko-leh</rt><rb>八</rb><rt>pah</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>多馬</rb><rt>To-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>門</rb><rt>Meng</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>關了個</rb><rt>kwæn-liao-go</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>立勒</rb><rt>lih-leh</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>Tsʽing</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>平安</rb><rt>bing-en</rt></ruby>！」<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>多馬</rb><rt>To-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>指頭</rb><rt>ts-deo</rt><rb>伸出來</rb><rt>sing-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>摸摸</rb><rt>moh-moh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>伸出來</rb><rt>sing-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>摸摸</rb><rt>moh-moh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>腰脅裏</rb><rt>iao-hyih-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>*z-ka*</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>總</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>。」<sup>28</sup> <u><ruby><rb>多馬</rb><rt>To-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>！」<sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>多馬</rb><rt>To-mô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>相信個</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>福氣</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>30</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>神蹟</rb><rt>jing-tsih</rt><rb>做過</rb><rt>tso-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇本</rb><rt>keh-peng</rt><rb>書裏</rb><rt>shü-li</rt><rb>寫的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>寫的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt></ruby>。

### Iʽe. XXI.

<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>KEH-SING</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>提比哩亞</rb><rt>Di-pi-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>邊沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>現出來</rb><rt>yin-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>現出來</rb><rt>yin-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>介個</rb><rt>ka-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西門</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u><u>·</u><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>多馬</rb><rt>To-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>低土馬</rb><rt>Ti-tʽu-mô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u> <ruby><rb><u>迦拿</u>個</rb><rt>Kyüô-nô-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拿但業</rb><rt>Nô-dæn-nyih</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西庇太</rb><rt>Si-pi-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>兩個</rb><rt>*liang-go*</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>*ng-ts*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>西門</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u><u>·</u><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>魚</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>隨手</rb><rt>ze-siu</rt><rb>跳落</rb><rt>tʽiao-lôh</rt><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>當夜</rb><rt>tông-yia</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>嘸告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>抲着</rb><rt>kʽô-djôh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>已經</rb><rt>Yi-kying</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>亮了</rb><rt>liang-de</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>立了</rb><rt>lih-leh</rt><rb>海塗上</rb><rt>hæ-du-zông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>5</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>小人</rb><rt>Siao-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>下飯</rb><rt>ʽô-væn</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>M̆-teh</rt></ruby>。」<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>船個</rb><rt>jün-go</rt><rb>順手邊</rb><rt>jing-siu-pin</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>下</rb><rt>ʽô</rt><rb>網</rb><rt>mông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>抲着</rb><rt>kʽô-djôh</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>下落去</rb><rt>ʽô-lôh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>網</rb><rt>mông</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>拖弗動</rb><rt>tʽo-feh-dong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>魚</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>緣故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>愛惜個</rb><rt>æ-sih-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>西門</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u><u>·</u><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>穿勒</rb><rt>cʽün-leh</rt><rb>布衫</rb><rt>pu-sæn</rt></ruby>，（<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>出膊個</rb><rt>cʽih-poh-go</rt></ruby>，）<ruby><rb>隨手</rb><rt>*ze-siu*</rt><rb>跳落</rb><rt>tʽiao-lôh</rt><rb>海裏</rb><rt>hæ-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>餘多個</rb><rt>Yü-to-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>駛</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>小船</rb><rt>siao-jün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拖勒</rb><rt>tʽo-leh</rt><rb>箇網</rb><rt>keh-mông</rt><rb>魚</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>攏來</rb><rt>long-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>離</rb><rt>li</rt><rb>岸</rb><rt>ngen</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>遠</rb><rt>yün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽô</rt><rb>廿把</rb><rt>nyiæn-pô</rt><rb>丈</rb><rt>dziang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>岸</rb><rt>ngen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>炭火</rb><rt>tʽæn-ho</rt><rb>生間</rb><rt>sang-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>上頭</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>魚</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>饅頭</rb><rt>mun-deo</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>纔始</rb><rt>Dzæ-s</rt><rb>抲着個</rb><rt>kʽô-djôh-go</rt><rb>魚</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>幾梗</rb><rt>kyi-kwang</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。」<sup>11</sup> <u><ruby><rb>西門</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u><u>·</u><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走落</rb><rt>tseo-lôh</rt><rb>箇隻</rb><rt>*keh-tsah*</rt><rb>小船裏</rb><rt>*siao-jün-li*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拖</rb><rt>tʽo</rt><rb>網</rb><rt>mông</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>岸</rb><rt>ngen</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>滿網</rb><rt>mun-mông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一百</rb><rt>ih-pah</rt><rb>五十</rb><rt>ng-jih</rt><rb>三梗</rb><rt>sæn-kwang</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>魚</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>雖即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>網</rb><rt>mông</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>裂開</rb><rt>lih-kʽæ</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>門徒</rb><rt>Meng-du</rt><rb>嘸人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>敢</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>饅頭</rb><rt>mun-deo</rt><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>魚</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介個</rb><rt>z-ka-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>活轉來</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>第三遭</rb><rt>di-sæn-tsao</rt><rb>現出來</rb><rt>yin-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>喫好</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-hao</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西門</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u><u>·</u><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb><u>約拿</u>個</rb><rt>Iah-nô-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>*ng-ts*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西門</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>*nying*</rt><rb>更加</rb><rt>keng-kô</rt><rb>愛敬</rb><rt>æ-kying</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>愛敬</rb><rt>æ-kying</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>餵</rb><rt>ü</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>siao-yiang</rt></ruby>。」<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>第二遭</rb><rt>Di-nyi-tsao</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb><u>約拿</u>個</rb><rt>Iah-nô-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西門</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>愛敬</rb><rt>æ-kying</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>愛敬</rb><rt>æ-kying</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>管</rb><rt>kwun</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>。」<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>第三遭</rb><rt>Di-sæn-tsao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb><u>約拿</u>個</rb><rt>Iah-nô-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西門</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>愛敬</rb><rt>æ-kying</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>第三遭</rb><rt>di-sæn-tsao</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>愛敬</rb><rt>æ-kying</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>憂悶</rb><rt>iu-meng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>樣樣</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>曉得個</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>愛敬</rb><rt>æ-kying</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>餵</rb><rt>ü</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真真</rb><rt>tsing-tsing</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>年紀</rb><rt>nyin-kyi</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>輕</rb><rt>kyʽing</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>記</rb><rt>kyi</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>繳身帶</rb><rt>*kyiao-sing-ta*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>年紀</rb><rt>nyin-kyi</rt><rb>老</rb><rt>lao</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>伸開</rb><rt>sing-kʽæ</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>別人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt><rb>縛勒</rb><rt>bo-leh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>扛</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。」<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇句</rb><rt>keh-kyü</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>指點</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>*Pe-teh*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>死法</rb><rt>si-fah</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>講好</rb><rt>Kông-hao</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>跟着</rb><rt>keng-djôh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>20</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>扭轉</rb><rt>nyin-cün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>愛惜個</rb><rt>æ-sih-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>跟的</rb><rt>keng-tih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>夜飯</rb><rt>yia-væn</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>戤到</rb><rt>gæ-tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶穌</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>胸脘頭</rb><rt>hyüong-kwun-deo</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>賣掉</rb><rt>ma-diao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」<sup>22</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>於</rb><rt>yü</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>相干</rb><rt>siang-ken</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>跟着</rb><rt>keng-djôh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是了</rb><rt>z-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>話開去了</rb><rt>wô-kʽæ-kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>𣍐</rb><rt>ve</rt><rb>死個</rb><rt>si-go</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>𣍐</rb><rt>ve</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>*z*</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>於</rb><rt>yü</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>相干</rb><rt>siang-ken</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>對證</rb><rt>TE-TSING</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>寫</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>實惠個</rb><rt>jih-we-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>25</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>做過</rb><rt>tso-ko</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>一打一</rb><rt>ih-tang-ih</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>寫落</rb><rt>sia-lôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>忖忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng-tsʽeng</rt><rb>寫落個</rb><rt>sia-lôh-go</rt><rb>書</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>囥弗過</rb><rt>kʽông-feh-ko</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>亞們</rb><rt>Üô-meng</rt></ruby>。
